   101	/**
   102	 * M1b: count the active filter axes for the `<details>` summary chip.
   103	 *
   104	 * The seven axes are:
   105	 *   1. tool          — active when ≠ "all"
   106	 *   2. storage       — active when ≠ "all"
   107	 *   3. status        — active when array is non-empty
   108	 *   4. project       — active when not null
   109	 *   5. search        — active when non-empty after trim
   110	 *   6. importableOnly — active when true
   111	 *   7. sort          — active when not deep-equal to the default
   112	 *                      `{field: "source_updated_at", direction: "desc"}`
   113	 *
   114	 * Exported so component tests + future call sites can consume the same
   115	 * helper without re-deriving the predicate. The chip suppresses at 0;
   116	 * non-zero renders as `${count} active`.
   117	 */
   118	export function countActiveFilters(filters: SessionFiltersState): number {
   119	  let count = 0;
   120	  if (filters.tool !== DEFAULT_FILTERS.tool) count += 1;
   121	  if (filters.storage !== DEFAULT_FILTERS.storage) count += 1;
   122	  if (filters.status.length > 0) count += 1;
   123	  if (filters.project !== null) count += 1;
   124	  if (filters.search.trim() !== "") count += 1;
   125	  if (filters.importableOnly === true) count += 1;
   126	  if (
   127	    filters.sort.field !== DEFAULT_FILTERS.sort.field ||
   128	    filters.sort.direction !== DEFAULT_FILTERS.sort.direction
   129	  ) {
   130	    count += 1;
   131	  }
   132	  return count;
   133	}
   134	
   135	/**
   136	 * M1b: hook that mirrors `App.tsx`'s narrow-mode listener pattern
   137	 * (matchMedia with `addEventListener("change", ...)` + legacy
   138	 * `addListener` fallback). Returns whether the media query currently
   139	 * matches. Default to the SSR-safe `false` when `window.matchMedia`
   140	 * is unavailable.
   141	 */
   142	function useMatchMedia(query: string): boolean {
   143	  const [matches, setMatches] = useState<boolean>(() => {
   144	    if (typeof window === "undefined") return false;
   145	    if (typeof window.matchMedia !== "function") return false;
   146	    return window.matchMedia(query).matches;
   147	  });
   148	  useEffect(() => {
   149	    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
   150	    if (typeof window.matchMedia !== "function") return;
   151	    const mq = window.matchMedia(query);
   152	    const onChange = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => setMatches(e.matches);
   153	    if (typeof mq.addEventListener === "function") {
   154	      mq.addEventListener("change", onChange);
   155	    } else if (
   156	      typeof (mq as unknown as { addListener?: typeof onChange }).addListener ===
   157	      "function"
   158	    ) {
   159	      (mq as unknown as { addListener: (l: typeof onChange) => void }).addListener(
   160	        onChange,
   161	      );
   162	    }
   163	    return () => {
   164	      if (typeof mq.removeEventListener === "function") {
   165	        mq.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
   166	      } else if (
   167	        typeof (mq as unknown as { removeListener?: typeof onChange })
   168	          .removeListener === "function"
   169	      ) {
   170	        (mq as unknown as { removeListener: (l: typeof onChange) => void }).removeListener(
   171	          onChange,
   172	        );
   173	      }
   174	    };
   175	  }, [query]);
   176	  return matches;
   177	}

   179	export function SessionFilters({
   180	  filters,
   181	  projects,
   182	  setFilter,
   183	  setImportableOnly,
   184	}: SessionFiltersProps) {
   185	  // M1b: spec-verbatim 1100 px breakpoint (NOT 1099.98 px) per the
   186	  // chunk dispatch brief. Above the breakpoint the filter strip
   187	  // renders inline; below it wraps in `<details>`.
   188	  const isWide = useMatchMedia("(min-width: 1100px)");
   189	
   190	  // The `<details>` open state. Default matches the current band
   191	  // (open above 1100 px; closed below). The intra-band preservation
   192	  // rule (per design.md §3.4 + UI/UX reviewer notes): when the user
   193	  // manually toggles the disclosure below 1100 px, that state must
   194	  // survive subsequent resize events that stay below 1100 px. ONLY
   195	  // crossing the breakpoint resets the open state to the new band's
   196	  // default. We track the previous `isWide` value and reset `open`
   197	  // only on a transition.
   198	  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState<boolean>(isWide);
   199	  const prevIsWideRef = useRef<boolean>(isWide);
   200	  useEffect(() => {
   201	    if (prevIsWideRef.current === isWide) return;
   202	    // Breakpoint crossed — reset to the new band's default.
   203	    prevIsWideRef.current = isWide;
   204	    setIsOpen(isWide);
   205	  }, [isWide]);
   206	
   207	  const activeCount = countActiveFilters(filters);
   208	
   209	  return (
   210	    <details
   211	      className="filters-wrap"
   212	      open={isOpen}
   213	      onToggle={(event) => {
   214	        // Honor the user's manual toggle below 1100 px. Above 1100 px
   215	        // the body is forced visible by CSS regardless of the `open`
   216	        // attribute (the summary is `display: none`), but we still
   217	        // sync state so the toggle event from a hidden summary does
   218	        // not stick the filter body in an unexpected state on the
   219	        // next resize crossing.
   220	        const next = (event.currentTarget as HTMLDetailsElement).open;
   221	        setIsOpen(next);
   222	      }}
   223	    >
   224	      <summary>
   225	        <span aria-hidden="true">Filters</span>
   226	        {activeCount > 0 ? (
   227	          <span className="filter-count-chip">{activeCount} active</span>
   228	        ) : null}
   229	      </summary>
   230	      <div className="session-filters" role="group" aria-label="Session filters">
   231	        <div className="filter-row">
   232	          <span className="filter-label">Tool</span>
   233	          {TOOL_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
   234	            <button
   235	              type="button"
   236	              key={`tool-${opt.value}`}
   237	              className={`chip${filters.tool === opt.value ? " active" : ""}`}
   238	              aria-pressed={filters.tool === opt.value}
   239	              onClick={() => setFilter("tool", opt.value)}
   240	            >
   241	              {opt.label}
   242	            </button>
   243	          ))}
   244	        </div>
   245	
   246	        <div className="filter-row">
   247	          <span className="filter-label">Storage</span>
   248	          {STORAGE_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
   249	            <button
   250	              type="button"
   251	              key={`storage-${opt.value}`}
   252	              className={`chip${filters.storage === opt.value ? " active" : ""}`}
   253	              aria-pressed={filters.storage === opt.value}
   254	              onClick={() => setFilter("storage", opt.value)}
   255	            >
   256	              {opt.label}
   257	            </button>
   258	          ))}
   259	        </div>
   260	
   261	        <div className="filter-row">
   262	          <span className="filter-label">Status</span>
   263	          <button
   264	            type="button"
   265	            className={`chip${filters.status.length === 0 ? " active" : ""}`}
   266	            aria-pressed={filters.status.length === 0}
   267	            onClick={() => setFilter("status", [])}
   268	          >
   269	            All
   270	          </button>
   271	          {STATUS_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
   272	            const active = filters.status.includes(opt.value);
   273	            return (
   274	              <button
   275	                type="button"
   276	                key={`status-${opt.value}`}
   277	                className={`chip${active ? " active" : ""}`}
   278	                aria-pressed={active}
   279	                onClick={() => {
   280	                  const next = active
   281	                    ? filters.status.filter((s) => s !== opt.value)
   282	                    : [...filters.status, opt.value];
   283	                  setFilter("status", next);
   284	                }}
   285	              >
   286	                {opt.label}
   287	              </button>
   288	            );
   289	          })}
   290	          <button
   291	            type="button"
   292	            className={`chip${filters.importableOnly ? " active" : ""}`}
   293	            aria-pressed={filters.importableOnly}
   294	            aria-label="Show importable only"
   295	            onClick={() => setImportableOnly(!filters.importableOnly)}
   296	          >
   297	            Importable only
   298	          </button>
   299	        </div>
   300

   302	          <label className="filter-label" htmlFor="session-filters-project">

   311	            title={filters.project ?? undefined}
   312	            onChange={(e) => {
   313	              const value = e.target.value;
   314	              setFilter("project", value === "" ? null : value);
   315	            }}
   316	          />
   317	          <datalist id="session-filters-project-list">
   318	            {projects.map((p) => (
   319	              <option key={p} value={p} />

   321	          </datalist>
   322	          {filters.project !== null ? (

     1	/*
     2	 * Feature-local CSS for the SessionFilters control bar.
     3	 *
     4	 * Imported by `SessionFilters.tsx` (sibling-import convention).
     5	 *
     6	 * Selectors owned here (M3 filter UI vocabulary, extended in M1b):
     7	 *   - `.filters-wrap` — Phase-5 / M1b `<details>` wrapper. Above
     8	 *     1100 px the disclosure chrome is hidden via CSS and the filter
     9	 *     body is forced visible (existing Phase-4 inline strip behavior).
    10	 *     Below 1100 px the `<summary>` becomes visible, the disclosure
    11	 *     toggles, and the body collapses by default.
    12	 *   - `.filters-wrap > summary` — chevron + "Filters" + active-count
    13	 *     chip; `cursor: pointer`, sienna `:focus-visible` ring with
    14	 *     2 px offset (parallel to `.back-to-list`), hairline bottom
    15	 *     border when `[open]`. The chevron is the browser-native
    16	 *     `::marker` triangle (no SVG, no icon library).
    17	 *   - `.filter-count-chip` — small muted pill, hairline border on
    18	 *     transparent ground; visually distinct from the filled
    19	 *     `.chip.active` so the chip reads as a passive indicator
    20	 *     (designer §7.7).
    21	 *   - `.session-filters` — column-with-gap layout for the chip groups,
    22	 *     project autocomplete, search, and sort selects.
    23	 *   - `.filter-row` — horizontal wrapping group within the bar.
    24	 *   - `.filter-label` — uppercase muted label that fronts each row.
    25	 *   - `.chip` — the small pill button; reused by status / tool /
    26	 *     storage / "Importable only" / clear-x affordances.
    27	 *   - `.chip:hover:not(:disabled)` — hover ring.
    28	 *   - `.chip.active` — active state; reuses the M1 badge color-mix
    29	 *     recipe (accent 15% bg + accent 70% fg + accent 35% border) so
    30	 *     it inherits the same WCAG AA contrast guarantee — measured at
    31	 *     light 6.237 / dark 6.949 (see progress log M6 contrast table).
    32	 *   - `.session-filters input`, `.session-filters select` — input
    33	 *     chrome; uses surface-raised so the inputs read as recessed
    34	 *     against the surrounding bar.
    35	 *   - `.session-filters input[type="text"]`, `[type="search"]` —
    36	 *     min-width clamp so the project autocomplete + substring search
    37	 *     inputs keep usable typing room when the bar wraps.
    38	 *
    39	 * Phase-5 / M1b structural literals (`working/phase-5.md` §Structural
    40	 * literals lines 926–946):
    41	 *   - `@media (min-width: 1100px)` — the filter strip wrap breakpoint
    42	 *     (spec line 930 verbatim).
    43	 *   - `border-radius: 999px` on `.chip` + `.filter-count-chip` (full
    44	 *     pill — `--radius-*` defines sm/md/lg, no "full"; same literal
    45	 *     reused by `.badge`).
    46	 *
    47	 * Disclosure-transition note (per spec line 1100 + design.md §3.4):
    48	 * NO custom CSS transition is applied to the `<details>` open/close.
    49	 * The `block-size`-via-`interpolate-size` exemption is reserved for
    50	 * M5's SkimView; M1b's filter strip is utility chrome and the
    51	 * editorial-instant snap is the correct gesture.
    52	 */
    53	
    54	.filters-wrap {
    55	  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);
    56	}

    33	
    34	// M1b matchMedia shim. happy-dom ships a basic `window.matchMedia`
    35	// stub that always returns `matches: false` and does not fire change
    36	// events; we override it here with a controllable mock so the tests
    37	// can drive the 1100 px breakpoint deterministically. Mirrors the
    38	// pattern used in App.test.tsx for the narrow-viewport listener.
    39	type MQListener = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => void;
    40	type MQEntry = {
    41	  query: string;
    42	  matches: boolean;
    43	  listeners: Set<MQListener>;
    44	};
    45	const mediaQueryRegistry = new Map<string, MQEntry>();
    46	
    47	function setMediaMatch(query: string, matches: boolean) {
    48	  const existing = mediaQueryRegistry.get(query);
    49	  if (existing && existing.matches === matches) return;
    50	  if (existing) {
    51	    existing.matches = matches;
    52	    // Fire change events on a clone so listeners that mutate the set
    53	    // (the cleanup paths in useEffect) do not corrupt iteration.
    54	    const event = { matches, media: query } as MediaQueryListEvent;
    55	    for (const listener of Array.from(existing.listeners)) {
    56	      listener(event);
    57	    }
    58	  } else {
    59	    mediaQueryRegistry.set(query, {
    60	      query,
    61	      matches,
    62	      listeners: new Set(),
    63	    });
    64	  }
    65	}
    66	
    67	const originalMatchMedia = (
    68	  globalThis as unknown as { window: { matchMedia?: typeof window.matchMedia } }
    69	).window.matchMedia;
    70	
    71	function installMatchMediaShim() {
    72	  (
    73	    globalThis as unknown as {
    74	      window: { matchMedia: (q: string) => MediaQueryList };
    75	    }
    76	  ).window.matchMedia = (query: string) => {
    77	    if (!mediaQueryRegistry.has(query)) {
    78	      mediaQueryRegistry.set(query, {
    79	        query,
    80	        matches: false,
    81	        listeners: new Set(),
    82	      });
    83	    }
    84	    const entry = mediaQueryRegistry.get(query)!;
    85	    return {
    86	      get matches() {
    87	        return entry.matches;
    88	      },
    89	      media: query,
    90	      onchange: null,
    91	      addListener: (l: MQListener) => entry.listeners.add(l),
    92	      removeListener: (l: MQListener) => entry.listeners.delete(l),
    93	      addEventListener: (_t: string, l: MQListener) => entry.listeners.add(l),
    94	      removeEventListener: (_t: string, l: MQListener) =>
    95	        entry.listeners.delete(l),
    96	      dispatchEvent: (_e: Event) => true,
    97	    } as unknown as MediaQueryList;
    98	  };
    99	}
   100	
   101	function restoreMatchMedia() {
   102	  if (originalMatchMedia) {
   103	    (
   104	      globalThis as unknown as {
   105	        window: { matchMedia?: typeof window.matchMedia };
   106	      }
   107	    ).window.matchMedia = originalMatchMedia;
   108	  }
   109	  mediaQueryRegistry.clear();
   110	}
   111	
   112	beforeEach(() => {
   113	  installMatchMediaShim();
   114	  // Default to "wide" — most tests assume the inline strip behavior.
   115	  setMediaMatch("(min-width: 1100px)", true);
   116	});
   117	
   118	afterEach(() => {
   119	  cleanup();
   120	  restoreMatchMedia();
   121	});
   122

   137	}

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/useSessionFilters.ts | sed -n '1,240p'" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Filter / sort / search state hook with versioned `localStorage`
     2	// persistence.
     3	//
     4	// Per `working/phase-4.md` §Filter, Sort, Search — Contract with the
     5	// Backend: the inspection surface persists a small filter blob under
     6	// `distill-portal:inspection-filters:v1`. The hook re-writes the blob on
     7	// every mutation so legacy / corrupt inputs heal naturally — the user
     8	// never sees an error toast for a malformed persisted shape.
     9	//
    10	// Robustness rules (covered by `useSessionFilters.test.ts`):
    11	//   - Parse failure (malformed JSON, null top-level, array top-level,
    12	//     string top-level) -> defaults applied + corrupt blob rewritten.
    13	//   - Missing top-level keys -> per-field defaults; valid keys accepted.
    14	//   - Unknown enum value for `tool` / `storage` / `sort.field` /
    15	//     `sort.direction` -> default for that key.
    16	//   - Non-array `status` -> default `[]`.
    17	//   - `status` array with one valid + one invalid entry -> drop the
    18	//     WHOLE array (apply default `[]`); do NOT silently filter to valid
    19	//     entries (per spec robustness clause).
    20	//   - Non-boolean `importableOnly` -> default `false`.
    21	//   - Out-of-range `pageSize` (anything not 50/100/200) -> default 50.
    22	//   - `project`: any string accepted on decode (it's dynamic data
    23	//     derived from the current row set; the decoder runs before the API
    24	//     loads so the value cannot be cross-checked here).
    25	//   - `localStorage` unavailable (private mode, quota, disabled, throws)
    26	//     -> in-memory fallback without surfacing an error.
    27	//
    28	// `importableOnly` interaction with `status` (per spec): when
    29	// `importableOnly === true`, the persisted `status` array is cleared on
    30	// apply (`setImportableOnly(true)`). The downstream filter
    31	// (`filterSessions.ts`) computes the EFFECTIVE status as
    32	// `["not_stored", "outdated"]` whenever `importableOnly === true`,
    33	// regardless of the persisted `status` array. This way the hook owns
    34	// the persistence rule and `filterSessions.ts` owns the apply-time
    35	// rule, matching the spec's "one filter mutation per affordance"
    36	// design (the "Show importable only" empty-state link sets a single
    37	// boolean rather than juggling a compound state).
    38	import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
    39	import type { SessionSyncStatus, Tool } from "../../lib/contracts";
    40	
    41	export const STORAGE_KEY = "distill-portal:inspection-filters:v1";
    42	
    43	export type SortField =
    44	  | "source_updated_at"
    45	  | "created_at"
    46	  | "ingested_at"
    47	  | "title"
    48	  | "project_path";
    49	
    50	export type SortDirection = "asc" | "desc";
    51	
    52	export type SessionFiltersState = {
    53	  tool: Tool | "all";
    54	  status: SessionSyncStatus[];
    55	  storage: "all" | "stored" | "not_stored";
    56	  importableOnly: boolean;
    57	  project: string | null;
    58	  search: string;
    59	  sort: { field: SortField; direction: SortDirection };
    60	  pageSize: 50 | 100 | 200;
    61	};
    62	
    63	export const DEFAULT_FILTERS: SessionFiltersState = {
    64	  tool: "all",
    65	  status: [],
    66	  storage: "all",
    67	  importableOnly: false,
    68	  project: null,
    69	  search: "",
    70	  sort: { field: "source_updated_at", direction: "desc" },
    71	  pageSize: 50,
    72	};
    73	
    74	const VALID_TOOLS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
    75	  "all",
    76	  "claude_code",
    77	  "codex",
    78	]);
    79	const VALID_STORAGES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
    80	  "all",
    81	  "stored",
    82	  "not_stored",
    83	]);
    84	const VALID_STATUSES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
    85	  "not_stored",
    86	  "up_to_date",
    87	  "outdated",
    88	  "source_missing",
    89	]);
    90	const VALID_SORT_FIELDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
    91	  "source_updated_at",
    92	  "created_at",
    93	  "ingested_at",
    94	  "title",
    95	  "project_path",
    96	]);
    97	const VALID_SORT_DIRECTIONS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(["asc", "desc"]);
    98	const VALID_PAGE_SIZES: ReadonlySet<number> = new Set([50, 100, 200]);
    99	
   100	/**
   101	 * Decode a raw `localStorage` value into a `SessionFiltersState`.
   102	 *
   103	 * Exported for tests; internal callers use `loadFilters` which combines
   104	 * decode + read in one step.
   105	 */
   106	export function decodeFilters(raw: string | null): SessionFiltersState {
   107	  if (raw === null) return { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS };
   108	  let parsed: unknown;
   109	  try {
   110	    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
   111	  } catch {
   112	    return { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS };
   113	  }
   114	  if (
   115	    parsed === null ||
   116	    typeof parsed !== "object" ||
   117	    Array.isArray(parsed)
   118	  ) {
   119	    return { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS };
   120	  }
   121	  const obj = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
   122	
   123	  const tool: SessionFiltersState["tool"] =
   124	    typeof obj.tool === "string" && VALID_TOOLS.has(obj.tool)
   125	      ? (obj.tool as SessionFiltersState["tool"])
   126	      : DEFAULT_FILTERS.tool;
   127	
   128	  // `status` rule: must be an array of valid SessionSyncStatus values.
   129	  // Any unknown entry drops the WHOLE array (per spec — do NOT silently
   130	  // filter to valid entries; defaulting to [] is the explicit choice so
   131	  // a corrupt blob cannot accidentally narrow the user's view).
   132	  let status: SessionSyncStatus[] = [...DEFAULT_FILTERS.status];
   133	  if (Array.isArray(obj.status)) {
   134	    let allValid = true;
   135	    for (const entry of obj.status) {
   136	      if (typeof entry !== "string" || !VALID_STATUSES.has(entry)) {
   137	        allValid = false;
   138	        break;
   139	      }
   140	    }
   141	    if (allValid) {
   142	      status = (obj.status as SessionSyncStatus[]).slice();
   143	    }
   144	  }
   145	
   146	  const storage: SessionFiltersState["storage"] =
   147	    typeof obj.storage === "string" && VALID_STORAGES.has(obj.storage)
   148	      ? (obj.storage as SessionFiltersState["storage"])
   149	      : DEFAULT_FILTERS.storage;
   150	
   151	  const importableOnly: boolean =
   152	    typeof obj.importableOnly === "boolean"
   153	      ? obj.importableOnly
   154	      : DEFAULT_FILTERS.importableOnly;
   155	
   156	  // `project` is dynamic data: any string accepted; null otherwise.
   157	  const project: string | null =
   158	    typeof obj.project === "string" ? obj.project : null;
   159	
   160	  const search: string =
   161	    typeof obj.search === "string" ? obj.search : DEFAULT_FILTERS.search;
   162	
   163	  // `sort` must be an object with a valid `field` and `direction`.
   164	  let sort: SessionFiltersState["sort"] = { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS.sort };
   165	  if (
   166	    obj.sort !== null &&
   167	    typeof obj.sort === "object" &&
   168	    !Array.isArray(obj.sort)
   169	  ) {
   170	    const sortObj = obj.sort as Record<string, unknown>;
   171	    const field: SortField =
   172	      typeof sortObj.field === "string" && VALID_SORT_FIELDS.has(sortObj.field)
   173	        ? (sortObj.field as SortField)
   174	        : DEFAULT_FILTERS.sort.field;
   175	    const direction: SortDirection =
   176	      typeof sortObj.direction === "string" &&
   177	      VALID_SORT_DIRECTIONS.has(sortObj.direction)
   178	        ? (sortObj.direction as SortDirection)
   179	        : DEFAULT_FILTERS.sort.direction;
   180	    sort = { field, direction };
   181	  }
   182	
   183	  const pageSize: 50 | 100 | 200 =
   184	    typeof obj.pageSize === "number" && VALID_PAGE_SIZES.has(obj.pageSize)
   185	      ? (obj.pageSize as 50 | 100 | 200)
   186	      : DEFAULT_FILTERS.pageSize;
   187	
   188	  return {
   189	    tool,
   190	    status,
   191	    storage,
   192	    importableOnly,
   193	    project,
   194	    search,
   195	    sort,
   196	    pageSize,
   197	  };
   198	}
   199	
   200	/** Best-effort `localStorage.getItem`. Returns null if storage is
   201	 *  unavailable (private mode, disabled, throws). */
   202	function safeGet(key: string): string | null {
   203	  try {
   204	    if (typeof globalThis.localStorage === "undefined") return null;
   205	    return globalThis.localStorage.getItem(key);
   206	  } catch {
   207	    return null;
   208	  }
   209	}
   210	
   211	/** Best-effort `localStorage.setItem`. Silent no-op on failure so the
   212	 *  in-memory fallback stays intact. */
   213	function safeSet(key: string, value: string): void {
   214	  try {
   215	    if (typeof globalThis.localStorage === "undefined") return;
   216	    globalThis.localStorage.setItem(key, value);
   217	  } catch {
   218	    // Quota exceeded, disabled, etc. — fall back to in-memory state.
   219	  }
   220	}
   221	
   222	/**
   223	 * `useSessionFilters` — owns the persisted filter blob and exposes a
   224	 * narrow setter API.
   225	 *
   226	 * Returned API:
   227	 *   - `filters`: the current `SessionFiltersState`.
   228	 *   - `setFilter<K>(key, value)`: replace a single field (and re-persist).
   229	 *   - `resetAll()`: drop every filter back to defaults (used by the
   230	 *     "Clear filters" empty-state affordance and the secondary
   231	 *     ActionBar caption's Clear button).
   232	 *   - `setImportableOnly(v)`: dedicated setter for the boolean shortcut.
   233	 *     When toggled to `true` it ALSO clears the persisted `status` array
   234	 *     (per spec: "Setting `importableOnly = true` overrides any
   235	 *     incompatible status array on apply"). When toggled to `false` it
   236	 *     leaves `status` untouched (the user's last array choice is
   237	 *     preserved).
   238	 */
   239	export function useSessionFilters(): {
   240	  filters: SessionFiltersState;

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba apps/frontend/src/App.tsx | sed -n '1,260p'" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Inspection page.
     2	//
     3	// Orchestrates three parallel fetches — source sessions, stored sessions,
     4	// scan errors — via `Promise.allSettled` so each panel settles
     5	// independently. A failure on one panel does not blank the others.
     6	// Each panel owns its own `{ loading, ok, error }` slice of state.
     7	//
     8	// As of Phase 4 Milestone 5 (Chunk F):
     9	//   - The list is PAGINATED client-side (page sizes 50 / 100 / 200,
    10	//     default 50; persisted via `useSessionFilters`). `pageIndex` is
    11	//     a separate piece of view state owned here, NOT in the filter
    12	//     hook (the hook persists user-settable filter axes; the page
    13	//     index is a transient "where am I in this scroll" value that
    14	//     should NOT survive a reload). The page slice math runs through
    15	//     `applyPagination` which also clamps the index, so a stale
    16	//     `pageIndex` from a shrunk row set self-heals on the next render.
    17	//   - Mutation outcomes (rescan + import) flow through a TOAST QUEUE
    18	//     (`useToastQueue` + `<Toast>`) instead of the M3-era inline
    19	//     `lastReport` text. Errors render a Retry action that re-derives
    20	//     the click-time intersection at retry time (mirrors the M3
    21	//     handleImport pattern; the same fresh re-derivation guards the
    22	//     pagination-cross-page race).
    23	//   - The Rescan button carries a "last rescan from this browser X
    24	//     ago" caption (read from / written to
    25	//     `distill-portal:last-manual-rescan:v1` per spec §Action Bar
    26	//     and Mutation UX). The caption is explicitly scoped to "this
    27	//     browser" because the backend runs its own scans the browser
    28	//     cannot observe.
    29	//
    30	// Selection model unchanged from M3: `selected: Set<string>` holds
    31	// backend `session_key` values; the `+K hidden by filters` caption
    32	// surfaces the raw-minus-effective gap; the click-time intersection
    33	// in `handleImport` continues to filter by the current FILTER
    34	// window (NOT the page window) so cross-page selection accumulated
    35	// through "Select all importable in current filter" still ships.
    36	//
    37	// The toast queue is rendered as a sibling of `<main>` for DOM
    38	// placement (the queue is a fixed-position overlay anchored bottom-
    39	// right). Note this sibling placement does NOT isolate React renders
    40	// — `useToastQueue` is App-level state, so push/dismiss re-renders
    41	// the entire App subtree. The reason the table render stays cheap is
    42	// memoization (`mergedRows` / `filteredRows` / `pageRows` are
    43	// `useMemo`d, so the heavy filter/sort/paginate chain doesn't re-run
    44	// on a toast push).
    45	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";

   122	
   123	  // M1a: deep-link pulse mechanism. If the URL pre-selected a row
   124	  // on initial mount, write the same row key into a separate slice
   125	  // so the matched list row applies `data-deep-link="true"` for one
   126	  // paint cycle. The cleanup path is "whichever fires first":
   127	  // either the row's `onAnimationEnd` clears it, or a 2 s safety
   128	  // timer clears it unconditionally (the row may never render in
   129	  // the session_not_found case). Click-driven selection NEVER fires

   131	  // captured ONCE on initial mount, not whenever selectedRowKey
   132	  // changes.
   133	  const [
   134	    pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey,

   137	  // Use a ref to the safety-timer ID so we can clear it from the
   138	  // animation-end path or from unmount.
   139	  const pulseTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
   140	  useEffect(() => {
   141	    // Only start the safety timer if there's a pulse target on
   142	    // initial mount. Subsequent state changes do not re-arm the
   143	    // pulse — the pulse mechanism is a one-shot per page load,
   144	    // matched against the URL on initial mount.
   145	    if (pendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey === null) return;
   146	    pulseTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   147	      setPendingDeepLinkPulseRowKey(null);
   148	      pulseTimerRef.current = null;
   149	    }, 2000);
   150	    return () => {
   151	      if (pulseTimerRef.current !== null) {
   152	        clearTimeout(pulseTimerRef.current);
   153	        pulseTimerRef.current = null;
   154	      }
   155	    };

   161	    );

   705	  useEffect(() => {
   706	    function isEditableTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
   707	      if (target === null) return false;
   708	      const el = target as HTMLElement;
   709	      // Tag check: input / textarea — both directly consume Esc
   710	      // semantics in their default behavior.
   711	      const tag = (el.tagName ?? "").toUpperCase();
   712	      if (tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA") return true;
   713	      // contenteditable elements: the plain `[contenteditable]`
   714	      // attribute selector matches every form HTML allows — the
   715	      // boolean attribute (`<div contenteditable>`), the empty-string
   716	      // form (`<div contenteditable="">`), the explicit "true" form,
   717	      // and the "plaintext-only" variant. Spec line 567 uses the
   718	      // plain selector for exactly this reason; codex's M1a fix-up #3
   719	      // pinned the previous narrow `'[contenteditable="true"]'`
   720	      // selector as a blocking finding because it missed the boolean
   721	      // / empty-string forms.
   722	      if (
   723	        typeof el.closest === "function" &&
   724	        el.closest("[contenteditable]") !== null
   725	      ) {
   726	        return true;
   727	      }
   728	      // ARIA combobox role — covers e.g. the future-typeahead
   729	      // autocomplete patterns.
   730	      if (
   731	        typeof el.closest === "function" &&
   732	        el.closest('[role="combobox"]') !== null
   733	      ) {
   734	        return true;
   735	      }
   736	      return false;
   737	    }
   738	    function isInScope(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
   739	      // Positive-scope rule (codex M1a fix-up #2): Esc only fires
   740	      // when the focus target is inside one of the two in-scope
   741	      // surfaces. Anywhere else the global Esc handler is a no-op,
   742	      // even on non-editable controls. This is the exact rule from
   743	      // spec line 567:
   744	      //   (a) descendant of `<article class="session-pane">`, OR
   745	      //   (b) on a selected row `<tr aria-current="true">`.
   746	      if (target === null) return false;
   747	      const el = target as HTMLElement;
   748	      if (typeof el.closest !== "function") return false;
   749	      if (el.closest("article.session-pane") !== null) return true;
   750	      if (el.closest('tr[aria-current="true"]') !== null) return true;
   751	      return false;

   753	    function onKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
   754	      if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
   755	      // (1) Negative scope: editable controls consume Esc themselves.

   757	      // (2) Positive scope: only fire when target is in-scope.

   763	      selectRow(null);
   764	      setNarrowMode("list");
   765	    }

    30	//                             button. The button mirrors the M1a
    31	//                             `.back-to-list` recipe (transparent
    32	//                             bg, no border, muted resting color,
    33	//                             underline on hover/focus, sienna
    34	//                             focus ring with 2 px offset). Clicking
    35	//                             it opens the still-mounted Phase-4
    36	//                             `<Drawer>` via the parent's
    37	//                             `onOpenDetail` callback. M2 will
    38	//                             delete this button when the Tabs
    39	//                             primitive ships — the surface is
    40	//                             intentionally low-anchor.
    41	//   - "session_not_found"   → the URL `?session=<key>` has no
    42	//                             matching row AFTER all three GETs
    43	//                             have settled. Two-line message +
    44	//                             two quiet buttons:
    45	//                               · "Clear selection" → selectRow(null)
    46	//                               · "Try Rescan"      → refetchAll()
    47	//                             Per spec line 572–574 the URL is NOT
    48	//                             auto-cleared — user pressing
    49	//                             "Clear selection" is the explicit
    50	//                             gesture.
    51	//
    52	// Stacked-narrow viewports (< 900 px) when narrowMode === "session"
    53	// also render the "← Back to list" quiet text-link button at the
    54	// top. Per Resolved Decision #17 (spec line 1162) "Back to list"
    55	// only sets narrowMode = "list" and PRESERVES selectedRowKey + URL
    56	// (so re-opening the same row brings the session pane back exactly
    57	// where the user left it). Esc is a different gesture: fully clears
    58	// selection. The handler for Esc lives globally in App.tsx.
    59	import "./SessionView.css";
    60	
    61	export type SessionViewState =
    62	  | "empty"
    63	  | "loading"
    64	  | "ready-placeholder"
    65	  | "session_not_found";
    66	
    67	export type SessionViewProps = {
    68	  state: SessionViewState;
    69	  /** When true (narrow viewport + narrowMode === "session"), render the "← Back to list" affordance. */
    70	  showBackToList: boolean;
    71	  /** Called when the user clicks "← Back to list". Sets narrowMode = "list" only. */
    72	  onBackToList: () => void;
    73	  /** Called when the user clicks "Clear selection" in the session_not_found state. */
    74	  onClearSelection: () => void;
    75	  /** Called when the user clicks "Try Rescan" in the session_not_found state. */
    76	  onTryRescan: () => void;
    77	  /** M1b: called when the user clicks the vestigial "Open detail"
    78	   *  button in the `ready-placeholder` state. The trigger element
    79	   *  (the button itself) is forwarded so the parent can stash it
    80	   *  in a ref and restore focus on close. Optional — when omitted,
    81	   *  the click is a no-op (defensive default for tests / future
    82	   *  M2 deletion path). */
    83	  onOpenDetail?: (triggerEl: HTMLElement | null) => void;
    84	};
    85	
    86	export function SessionView({
    87	  state,
    88	  showBackToList,
    89	  onBackToList,
    90	  onClearSelection,
    91	  onTryRescan,
    92	  onOpenDetail,
    93	}: SessionViewProps) {
    94	  return (
    95	    <article
    96	      className="session-pane"
    97	      data-state={state}
    98	      aria-busy={state === "loading"}
    99	      aria-live="polite"
   100	    >
   101	      {showBackToList ? (
   102	        <button
   103	          type="button"
   104	          className="back-to-list"
   105	          onClick={onBackToList}
   106	        >
   107	          ← Back to list
   108	        </button>
   109	      ) : null}
   110	      <div className="session-state">
   111	        {state === "empty" ? <EmptyPaneCopy /> : null}
   112	        {state === "loading" ? <LoadingCopy /> : null}
   113	        {state === "ready-placeholder" ? (
   114	          <ReadyPlaceholderCopy onOpenDetail={onOpenDetail} />
   115	        ) : null}
   116	        {state === "session_not_found" ? (
   117	          <SessionNotFoundCopy
   118	            onClearSelection={onClearSelection}
   119	            onTryRescan={onTryRescan}
   120	          />
   121	        ) : null}
   122	      </div>
   123	    </article>
   124	  );
   125	}
   126	
   127	/**
   128	 * Empty preface — verbatim spec lines 591–593. The mark glyph is a
   129	 * typographic ornament (a centered middle-dot) at `var(--text-xl)`
   130	 * — designer §7.1 picked text-only over an SVG illustration to
   131	 * match the Archive-room "ink-on-paper" mood AND avoid a new asset
   132	 * dependency. NB: the spec's narrative once mentioned `--text-2xl`
   133	 * but that is an M2 typography token; M1a uses `--text-xl` (Phase-4
   134	 * baseline) and the round-1 UI/UX reviewer pinned this as a
   135	 * blocking finding in the design loop.
   136	 */
   137	function EmptyPaneCopy() {
   138	  return (
   139	    <>
   140	      <span className="empty-mark" aria-hidden="true">
   141	        ·
   142	      </span>
   143	      <p className="empty-prose-1">
   144	        Select a session from the list to view its content.
   145	      </p>
   146	      <p className="empty-prose-2">
   147	        The session view shows the full Transcript chronologically, a
   148	        Skim outline (one block per user message), the Raw NDJSON for
   149	        verification, and the session's Metadata.
   150	      </p>
   151	    </>
   152	  );
   153	}
   154	
   155	function LoadingCopy() {
   156	  return <p className="loading-line">Reading session…</p>;
   157	}
   158	
   159	function ReadyPlaceholderCopy({
   160	  onOpenDetail,
   161	}: {
   162	  onOpenDetail?: (triggerEl: HTMLElement | null) => void;
   163	}) {
   164	  return (
   165	    <>
   166	      <p className="placeholder-line">
   167	        Session view coming in Milestone 2.
   168	      </p>
   169	      {/* M1b vestigial "Open detail" text-link button. Renders ONLY
   170	       * in the `ready-placeholder` state (not in empty / loading /
   171	       * session_not_found). The Phase-4 `<Drawer>` + `<SessionDetail>`
   172	       * remain mounted; clicking this button is the M1b drawer
   173	       * entry point (row click no longer auto-mounts the drawer).
   174	       * The button is intentionally low-anchor; M2's Tabs primitive
   175	       * will replace it. */}
   176	      <button
   177	        type="button"
   178	        className="open-detail"
   179	        onClick={(event) => {
   180	          if (onOpenDetail) onOpenDetail(event.currentTarget);
   181	        }}
   182	      >
   183	        Open detail
   184	      </button>
   185	    </>
   186	  );
   187	}
   188	
   189	function SessionNotFoundCopy({
   190	  onClearSelection,
   191	  onTryRescan,
   192	}: {
   193	  onClearSelection: () => void;
   194	  onTryRescan: () => void;
   195	}) {
   196	  return (
   197	    <>
   198	      <h2 className="not-found-heading">Session not found in current view</h2>
   199	      <p className="not-found-hint">
   200	        The session referenced by the URL was not in the merged set
   201	        after the latest scan.
   202	      </p>
   203	      <div className="not-found-actions">
   204	        <button
   205	          type="button"
   206	          className="quiet-button"
   207	          onClick={onClearSelection}
   208	        >
   209	          Clear selection
   210	        </button>
   211	        <button
   212	          type="button"
   213	          className="quiet-button"
   214	          onClick={onTryRescan}
   215	        >
   216	          Try Rescan
   217	        </button>
   218	      </div>
   219	    </>
   220	  );
   221	}

   300	  }, [sourceState, storedState]);
   301	
   302	  // Filter + sort pipeline. `applyFilters` and `applySort` are pure;
   303	  // they accept new state objects each call, so wrapping the chain in
   304	  // `useMemo` is the natural way to keep render cost flat across
   305	  // re-renders that don't touch the merged set or the filter state.
   306	  const filteredRows = useMemo(
   307	    () => applySort(applyFilters(mergedRows, filters), filters.sort),
   308	    [mergedRows, filters],
   309	  );
   310	
   311	  // Distinct project paths in the current MERGED set (not the filtered
   312	  // set) so the project autocomplete still surfaces every project even
   313	  // when another filter narrowed the view.
   314	  const projects = useMemo(
   315	    () => distinctProjectPaths(mergedRows),
   316	    [mergedRows],
   317	  );
   318	
   319	  // Pagination: derive the page slice + the clamped page index. The
   320	  // `applyPagination` helper guarantees pageIndex is in
   321	  // `[0, totalPages-1]` so a stale state value can't render an empty
   322	  // page when filteredRows is non-empty. The clamped value is
   323	  // synced back to React state via the effect below so subsequent
   324	  // renders agree on the same index.
   325	  const {
   326	    pageRows,
   327	    pageIndex: clampedPageIndex,
   328	  } = useMemo(
   329	    () => applyPagination(filteredRows, pageIndex, filters.pageSize),
   330	    [filteredRows, pageIndex, filters.pageSize],
   331	  );
   332	
   333	  // Sync the clamped pageIndex back to state if the helper had to
   334	  // adjust it (e.g. a filter mutation shrank the row set so the
   335	  // user's last pageIndex now lands past totalPages). The
   336	  // `clampedPageIndex !== pageIndex` guard prevents an infinite
   337	  // setState loop.
   338	  useEffect(() => {
   339	    if (clampedPageIndex !== pageIndex) {
   340	      setPageIndex(clampedPageIndex);
   341	    }
   342	  }, [clampedPageIndex, pageIndex]);
   343	
   344	  // Page-reset on filter change. Per spec §localStorage robustness:
   345	  // "Changing any filter or the sort resets the page to 1." We list
   346	  // every filter axis explicitly so a future addition has to opt in
   347	  // (and we don't accidentally reset on an unrelated render). We do
   348	  // NOT list `filters.pageSize` here — that change is handled by the
   349	  // recompute effect below so the first visible row stays visible.
   350	  useEffect(() => {
   351	    setPageIndex(0);
   352	  }, [
   353	    filters.tool,
   354	    filters.status,
   355	    filters.storage,
   356	    filters.importableOnly,
   357	    filters.project,
   358	    filters.search,
   359	    filters.sort.field,
   360	    filters.sort.direction,
   361	  ]);
   362	
   363	  // Page-size change recompute. Per spec §localStorage robustness:
   364	  // "Changing the page size recomputes the current page so the first
   365	  // visible row stays visible if possible." If the user is on page 2
   366	  // with pageSize 50 (rows 50-99) and switches to pageSize 100, the
   367	  // new pageIndex must be 0 (rows 0-99 — keeps row 50 visible);
   368	  // switching back to pageSize 50 from there returns them to page 1
   369	  // (rows 50-99). The math is: firstVisibleRow = pageIndex * oldSize;
   370	  // newPageIndex = floor(firstVisibleRow / newSize).
   371	  useEffect(() => {
   372	    const oldSize = prevPageSizeRef.current;
   373	    const newSize = filters.pageSize;
   374	    if (oldSize !== newSize) {
   375	      // Use a functional setState so we read the freshest pageIndex
   376	      // (not a closure-captured one).
   377	      setPageIndex((prev) => Math.floor((prev * oldSize) / newSize));
   378	      prevPageSizeRef.current = newSize;
   379	    }
   380	  }, [filters.pageSize]);
   381	
   382	  // Visible importable set under the CURRENT filter window. The
   383	  // click-time intersection in `handleImport` reads from this same set
   384	  // so the POST body never contains a row hidden by filter. NOTE:
   385	  // intentionally NOT scoped to `pageRows` — pagination must NOT
   386	  // narrow the bulk-select target (per spec §Pagination vs
   387	  // virtualization risk: cross-page selection accumulated through
   388	  // "Select all importable in current filter" must still ship).
   389	  const visibleImportableInFilter = useMemo(() => {
   390	    const set = new Set<string>();
   391	    for (const row of filteredRows) {
   392	      if (isImportable(row) && row.sourceSessionKey !== null) {
   393	        set.add(row.sourceSessionKey);
   394	      }
   395	    }
   396	    return set;
   397	  }, [filteredRows]);
   398	
   399	  // Visible importable set across the FULL merged set (i.e. ignoring the
   400	  // filter window). Used to compute the "+K hidden by filters" caption:
   401	  // a key is "hidden by filter" when it's in `selected`, importable in
   402	  // the full merged set, but NOT importable in the current filter
   403	  // window.
   404	  const visibleImportableInMerged = useMemo(() => {
   405	    const set = new Set<string>();
   406	    for (const row of mergedRows) {
   407	      if (isImportable(row) && row.sourceSessionKey !== null) {
   408	        set.add(row.sourceSessionKey);
   409	      }
   410	    }
   411	    return set;
   412	  }, [mergedRows]);
   413	
   414	  const handleToggleAll = useCallback(() => {
   415	    setSelected((prev) => {
   416	      // Toggle bulk-select against the CURRENT FILTER window. A user
   417	      // who narrows the view and clicks the header checkbox expects to
   418	      // select only what they can see (per spec § Bulk-select
   419	      // affordances: "Select all importable in current filter"). This
   420	      // is NOT scoped to the current page — the cross-page selection
   421	      // is the whole point of the affordance.
   422	      const importableKeys = Array.from(visibleImportableInFilter);
   423	      const allSelected =
   424	        importableKeys.length > 0 &&
   425	        importableKeys.every((k) => prev.has(k));
   426	      if (allSelected) {
   427	        // Clear only the keys that came from the current filter
   428	        // window; selection from outside the filter (the +K hidden
   429	        // by filters) is left alone — Clear hidden / Clear selection
   430	        // are the dedicated affordances for those.
   431	        const next = new Set(prev);
   432	        for (const k of importableKeys) next.delete(k);
   433	        return next;
   434	      }
   435	      const next = new Set(prev);
   436	      for (const k of importableKeys) next.add(k);
   437	      return next;
   438	    });
   439	  }, [visibleImportableInFilter]);
   440	
   441	  // Refs for the latest handleRescan / handleImport. Toast `onRetry`
   442	  // closures capture these refs (NOT the handlers directly), so a
   443	  // Retry click always invokes the LATEST handler — which re-derives
   444	  // the click-time intersection from current state, not from the
   445	  // state that was live when the failure-toast was first pushed.
   446	  // Without this, a Retry click after a rescan-between-attempts
   447	  // would read the pre-rescan `selected` / `sourceState` snapshot.
   448	  const handleRescanRef = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.resolve());
   449	  const handleImportRef = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(() => Promise.resolve());
   450	
   451	  const handleRescan = useCallback(async () => {
   452	    setPending("rescan");
   453	    try {
   454	      const report: RescanReport = await triggerRescan();
   455	      // Persist the success timestamp + push to local state so the
   456	      // ActionBar caption updates immediately. Per spec, the caption
   457	      // is explicitly scoped to "this browser" so we ONLY write on
   458	      // the success path; an error keeps the previous timestamp.
   459	      const iso = new Date().toISOString();
   460	      writeLastRescan(iso);
   461	      setLastRescanAt(iso);
   462	      pushToast({
   463	        kind: "success",
   464	        title: "Rescan complete",
   465	        message: rescanSummary(report),
   466	        details: rescanCounts(report),
   467	      });
   468	      await refetchAll();
   469	    } catch (error) {
   470	      pushToast({
   471	        kind: "error",
   472	        title: "Rescan failed",
   473	        message: messageFor(error),
   474	        onRetry: () => {
   475	          // Re-derive the rescan call at click time via the ref so
   476	          // we always invoke the LATEST handleRescan — not the
   477	          // closure-captured one from the render that pushed this
   478	          // toast. Fire-and-forget; the inner handler will push its
   479	          // own success/error toast.
   480	          void handleRescanRef.current();
   481	        },
   482	      });
   483	    } finally {
   484	      setPending(null);
   485	    }
   486	  }, [refetchAll, pushToast]);
   487	
   488	  const handleImport = useCallback(async () => {
   489	    setPending("import");
   490	    // Derive the import payload at click time from the currently-
   491	    // FILTERED rows that are still importable. Three defenses bake
   492	    // into one expression:
   493	    //   (a) F2 visible-intersection: a rescan that has just pruned a
   494	    //       row from the source list won't ship its key, even if the
   495	    //       reconciliation `useEffect` has not yet flushed.
   496	    //   (b) M2 importability: a row that has ALWAYS had a non-null
   497	    //       `sourceSessionKey` but is `up_to_date` (i.e. visible but
   498	    //       not eligible for import) won't ship either, even if the
   499	    //       UI somehow leaked a stale identity into `selected`.
   500	    //   (c) M3 filter-window: a row that the user selected, then
   501	    //       hid via a filter mutation, will NOT ship — only rows in
   502	    //       the current filter window are eligible.
   503	    //   (d) M5 cross-page: pagination does NOT narrow the eligible
   504	    //       set; a row selected on page 1 but currently displayed on
   505	    //       page 2 still ships if it remains in the filter window.
   506	    //       The filter pipeline is the gate, not pagination.
   507	    // The same `isImportable` helper used by SessionsTable governs
   508	    // (a)+(b), and the filter pipeline owns (c)+(d), so the rendered
   509	    // checkbox set, the action-bar count, and the POST body cannot
   510	    // drift apart.
   511	    //
   512	    // Build the visible-importable set fresh inside the handler so
   513	    // we read whatever state landed up to the moment of the click,
   514	    // not a value captured by `useMemo` at the previous render. This
   515	    // mirrors the F2 pattern where the click happens during a
   516	    // commit-time scheduler race; a stale memoized closure here
   517	    // would re-open the bug.
   518	    const src = sourceState.kind === "ok" ? sourceState.data : [];
   519	    const stor = storedState.kind === "ok" ? storedState.data : [];
   520	    const liveMerged = mergeSessions(src, stor);
   521	    const liveFiltered = applySort(
   522	      applyFilters(liveMerged, filters),
   523	      filters.sort,
   524	    );
   525	    const liveVisibleImportable = new Set<string>();
   526	    for (const row of liveFiltered) {
   527	      if (isImportable(row) && row.sourceSessionKey !== null) {
   528	        liveVisibleImportable.add(row.sourceSessionKey);
   529	      }
   530	    }
   531	    const keysToImport = Array.from(selected).filter((k) =>
   532	      liveVisibleImportable.has(k),
   533	    );
   534	    try {
   535	      const report: ImportReport = await importSourceSessions(keysToImport);
   536	      setSelected(new Set());
   537	      pushToast({
   538	        kind: "success",
   539	        title: "Import complete",
   540	        message: importSummary(report),
   541	        details: importCounts(report),
   542	      });
   543	      await refetchAll();
   544	    } catch (error) {
   545	      pushToast({
   546	        kind: "error",
   547	        title: "Import failed",
   548	        message: messageFor(error),
   549	        onRetry: () => {
   550	          // Per spec: "Error/retry: if Import fails and the user
   551	          // retries, the retry re-derives the effective selection at
   552	          // click time — if a rescan fired between attempts, the
   553	          // payload reflects the new state, never the pre-error
   554	          // cache." We dispatch through the ref so the click invokes
   555	          // the LATEST handleImport — its useCallback deps include
   556	          // `selected`, `sourceState`, `storedState`, `filters`, so
   557	          // the function identity changes whenever any of those does.
   558	          // Without the ref hop, this closure would capture the
   559	          // handler from the render that pushed the toast and would
   560	          // re-derive against THAT handler's stale closure scope.
   561	          void handleImportRef.current();
   562	        },
   563	      });
   564	    } finally {
   565	      setPending(null);
   566	    }
   567	  }, [selected, sourceState, storedState, filters, refetchAll, pushToast]);
   568	
   569	  // Keep the refs pointed at the LATEST handler identities so toast
   570	  // Retry closures (pushed earlier) always re-invoke the current
   571	  // versions — see comment on `handleRescanRef` / `handleImportRef`
   572	  // above for the rationale.
   573	  useEffect(() => {
   574	    handleRescanRef.current = handleRescan;
   575	  }, [handleRescan]);
   576	  useEffect(() => {
   577	    handleImportRef.current = handleImport;
   578	  }, [handleImport]);
   579	
   580	  // Selected count for the action bar mirrors the same intersection
   581	  // the import POST will use, so "Import selected (N)" always names
   582	  // the exact set that would ship if clicked now.
   583	  let selectedCount = 0;
   584	  for (const k of selected) {
   585	    if (visibleImportableInFilter.has(k)) selectedCount += 1;
   586	  }
   587	
   588	  // "+K hidden by filters" caption: keys the user has selected that
   589	  // are still importable in the merged set but NOT in the filter
   590	  // window. These are rows that fell out of the user's current view
   591	  // because of a filter, not because the backend pruned them.
   592	  let hiddenByFilterCount = 0;
   593	  for (const k of selected) {
   594	    if (
   595	      visibleImportableInMerged.has(k) &&
   596	      !visibleImportableInFilter.has(k)
   597	    ) {
   598	      hiddenByFilterCount += 1;
   599	    }
   600	  }
   601	
   602	  const onClearHidden = useCallback(() => {
   603	    setSelected((prev) => {
   604	      const next = new Set<string>();
   605	      for (const k of prev) {
   606	        if (visibleImportableInFilter.has(k)) next.add(k);
   607	      }
   608	      return next;
   609	    });
   610	  }, [visibleImportableInFilter]);
   611	
   612	  const onClearSelection = useCallback(() => {
   613	    setSelected(new Set());
   614	  }, []);
   615	
   616	  const onChangePage = useCallback((next: number) => {
   617	    setPageIndex(next);
   618	  }, []);
   619	
   620	  const onChangePageSize = useCallback(
   621	    (size: PageSize) => {
   622	      setFilter("pageSize", size);
   623	    },
   624	    [setFilter],
   625	  );
   626	
   627	  // M1a: row-click → URL-synced selection. The Phase-4 row-click →
   628	  // drawer flow ALSO fires on the same click (preserved in M1a;
   629	  // M1b retires it); the selection setter here only controls the
   630	  // right pane + URL. Narrow viewports also flip narrowMode to
   631	  // "session" so the user sees the session pane after the click.
   632	  const handleSelectRow = useCallback(
   633	    (rowKey: string) => {
   634	      selectRow(rowKey);
   635	      setNarrowMode("session");
   636	    },
   637	    [selectRow],
   638	  );
   639	
   640	  // M1a: "← Back to list" gesture. Per Resolved Decision #17 (spec
   641	  // line 1162) this only sets `narrowMode = "list"` — does NOT
   642	  // clear `selectedRowKey` and does NOT clear the URL. Esc is the
   643	  // distinct gesture for fully clearing.
   644	  const handleBackToList = useCallback(() => {
   645	    setNarrowMode("list");
   646	  }, []);
   647	
   648	  // M1a: Try Rescan from the session_not_found state. Fires the
   649	  // standard refetch — does NOT clear the URL (preserves the deep
   650	  // link in case Rescan finds the row).
   651	  const handleTryRescan = useCallback(() => {
   652	    void refetchAll();
   653	  }, [refetchAll]);
   654	
   655	  // M1b: ref handle into SessionsView's drawer-trigger flow. The
   656	  // vestigial "Open detail" button in `SessionView` invokes
   657	  // `handleOpenDetailFromSessionView` below; that callback resolves
   658	  // the current `selectedRowKey` against the merged set and
   659	  // delegates to `SessionsView.openDetail`. Drawer state stays
   660	  // encapsulated in `SessionsView` (Phase 4 pattern preserved per
   661	  // the planner's recommendation; minimizes diff vs lifting it
   662	  // entirely into App.tsx).
   663	  const sessionsViewRef = useRef<SessionsViewHandle | null>(null);
   664	  const handleOpenDetailFromSessionView = useCallback(
   665	    (triggerEl: HTMLElement | null) => {
   666	      if (selectedRowKey === null) return;
   667	      const handle = sessionsViewRef.current;
   668	      if (handle === null) return;
   669	      handle.openDetail(selectedRowKey, triggerEl);
   670	    },
   671	    [selectedRowKey],
   672	  );
   673	
   674	  // M1a: Clear selection from the session_not_found state.
   675	  // Identical to Esc's effect on the URL: clears selectedRowKey
   676	  // and removes the `?session=` query param. Does NOT clear
   677	  // narrowMode (the user is on a narrow viewport ⇒ already
   678	  // visible-mode "session" but now empty; we flip to "list" so they
   679	  // see the list).
   680	  const handleClearSelectionFromNotFound = useCallback(() => {
   681	    selectRow(null);
   682	    setNarrowMode("list");
   683	  }, [selectRow]);
   684	
   685	  // M1a: global `Esc` handler. Clears the URL `?session=` slice +
   686	  // selectedRowKey + narrowMode in a single keystroke. Per spec
   687	  // line 567 + Resolved Decision #17, the handler is SCOPED in two
   688	  // independent ways:
   689	  //   (1) NEGATIVE scope (editable-control filter): ignored when
   690	  //       focus is in `<input>`, `<textarea>`, any `[contenteditable]`
   691	  //       (any value, including the boolean / empty / "plaintext-only"
   692	  //       forms — codex M1a fix-up #3), or any `[role="combobox"]`.
   693	  //       Editable controls get to consume Esc themselves first
   694	  //       (clear search, dismiss combobox, etc.).
   695	  //   (2) POSITIVE scope (in-scope surfaces only — codex M1a fix-up
   696	  //       #2): even when focus is non-editable, Esc fires ONLY when
   697	  //       the target is (a) a descendant of `<article class="session-
   698	  //       pane">` OR (b) the currently-selected list row
   699	  //       (`<tr aria-current="true">`). Esc is IGNORED everywhere
   700	  //       else (filter buttons, table headers, action-bar checkboxes,

exec

   254	test("SessionView back-to-list narrow-viewport visibility: global.css carries the @media override that re-enables `display`", async () => {

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/useSelectedSession.ts | sed -n '1,220p'" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// URL-synced selection hook for the inspection master-detail layout.
     2	//
     3	// Owns the `?session=<rowKey>` slice of the page URL. React state is
     4	// the single source of truth; the URL is a mirror written via
     5	// `window.history.replaceState` (NOT `pushState` — see Resolved
     6	// Decision #1 in `working/phase-5.md` lines 1142, 559–586). Casual
     7	// scanning across 20 sessions doesn't pollute the back-stack; deep-
     8	// link bookmarkability still works because the URL reflects the
     9	// current selection.
    10	//
    11	// Coordination rules (so popstate / click / Esc never race — spec
    12	// lines 563–568):
    13	//
    14	//   - selectRow(key)  → setState(key) AND replaceState(buildUrl(key)).
    15	//                       Both are idempotent.
    16	//   - popstate event  → re-read URL, setState directly. Does NOT
    17	//                       call replaceState (would feedback-loop with
    18	//                       the browser's own URL push).
    19	//   - buildUrl(key)   → reads the entire current URLSearchParams,
    20	//                       mutates ONLY the `session` key (sets it to
    21	//                       `key` or removes it if `null`), serializes
    22	//                       back. Preserves all other query params for
    23	//                       forward-compat with future filter URL state.
    24	//
    25	// Initial mount reads `URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(
    26	// "session")` and pre-selects so a deep-linked URL reload restores
    27	// the selection. The popstate listener is cleaned up on unmount.
    28	import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
    29	
    30	export type UseSelectedSession = {
    31	  selectedRowKey: string | null;
    32	  selectRow: (rowKey: string | null) => void;
    33	};
    34	
    35	/**
    36	 * Build the next URL after mutating `session` to `key` (or removing
    37	 * it when `key === null`). Reads the entire current
    38	 * `window.location.search`, updates only the `session` key, and
    39	 * serializes back. Preserves every other query param — the future
    40	 * filter URL state slice owns its own keys; we must not stomp on
    41	 * them.
    42	 *
    43	 * Returns the full URL (pathname + search + hash) so callers can
    44	 * pass it directly to `history.replaceState`.
    45	 */
    46	export function buildUrl(key: string | null): string {
    47	  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    48	  if (key === null) {
    49	    params.delete("session");
    50	  } else {
    51	    params.set("session", key);
    52	  }
    53	  const search = params.toString();
    54	  const path = window.location.pathname;
    55	  const hash = window.location.hash;
    56	  return `${path}${search.length > 0 ? `?${search}` : ""}${hash}`;
    57	}
    58	
    59	function readSessionFromUrl(): string | null {
    60	  return new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("session");
    61	}
    62	
    63	export function useSelectedSession(): UseSelectedSession {
    64	  // Initial mount reads the URL once. `useState` with a lazy
    65	  // initializer so the URL read happens exactly once at mount time
    66	  // (per-render evaluation would risk reading after a popstate set
    67	  // state but before the listener fired).
    68	  const [selectedRowKey, setSelectedRowKey] = useState<string | null>(() =>
    69	    readSessionFromUrl(),
    70	  );
    71	
    72	  // Stable callback identity. The handler closes over `setState`,
    73	  // which is itself stable, so `selectRow` does not need to depend
    74	  // on any state. Consumers can pass it into `useEffect` deps
    75	  // arrays without re-arming the effect on every render.
    76	  const selectRow = useCallback((rowKey: string | null) => {
    77	    setSelectedRowKey(rowKey);
    78	    // Mirror to URL. `replaceState(null, "", url)` is idempotent —
    79	    // calling it with the same URL is a no-op per the History API
    80	    // spec. We do NOT call it from the popstate handler (the
    81	    // browser already updated the URL; calling replaceState there
    82	    // would feedback-loop).
    83	    window.history.replaceState(null, "", buildUrl(rowKey));
    84	  }, []);
    85	
    86	  // popstate listener: browser Back / Forward fires this. Re-read
    87	  // the URL and sync state ONLY (no replaceState — that would
    88	  // feedback-loop). Use a ref-shaped pattern via the effect's
    89	  // closure so the listener identity stays stable across renders.
    90	  // The listener is registered exactly once on mount + removed on
    91	  // unmount.
    92	  const popstateRef = useRef<((event: PopStateEvent) => void) | null>(null);
    93	  useEffect(() => {
    94	    const handler = (_event: PopStateEvent) => {
    95	      const next = readSessionFromUrl();
    96	      setSelectedRowKey(next);
    97	    };
    98	    popstateRef.current = handler;
    99	    window.addEventListener("popstate", handler);
   100	    return () => {
   101	      window.removeEventListener("popstate", handler);
   102	      popstateRef.current = null;
   103	    };
   104	  }, []);
   105	
   106	  return { selectedRowKey, selectRow };
   107	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Generic shell that wraps the native HTML `<dialog>` element.
     2	//
     3	// Why native `<dialog>` instead of a hand-rolled modal:
     4	//   - The platform delegates focus-trap, Esc-close, and focus-restoration
     5	//     semantics so we do not have to re-implement them. Hand-rolled focus
     6	//     traps are a recurring source of a11y bugs and are not worth
     7	//     re-implementing in Phase 4.
     8	//   - One drawer at a time falls out of `showModal()` semantics: a second
     9	//     `showModal()` call on a different `<dialog>` raises
    10	//     `InvalidStateError` in real Chromium, which is the correct
    11	//     behaviour. The Phase 4 spec is explicit ("One drawer at a time").
    12	//
    13	// Per `working/phase-4.md` §Dependency Policy the only browser target is
    14	// Chromium; the documented escape hatch (a focus-management package)
    15	// fires only if the Playwright real-Chromium reproducer fails. THIS
    16	// CHUNK FIRED THE ESCAPE HATCH:
    17	//
    18	//   Reproducer (apps/frontend/e2e/inspection.spec.ts step 9): with a
    19	//   3-focusable-element drawer (Close, Copy path, View raw), open the
    20	//   drawer via Enter on a row, then press Tab repeatedly. After Tab
    21	//   #3 the activeElement leaves the dialog subtree (escapes to BODY),
    22	//   meaning native `<dialog>` does NOT cycle Tab back to the first
    23	//   focusable element. This contradicts the platform's "inert outside,
    24	//   trap inside" expectation that the spec leaned on. Captured as
    25	//   "focus-trap broke after Tab #3; activeElement: BODY" in the chunk
    26	//   evidence pack.
    27	//
    28	// Mitigation per spec: add `focus-trap-react` (one small focus-
    29	// management package) and wrap the dialog children. Component tests
    30	// run under happy-dom; happy-dom does NOT implement Tab cycling, so
    31	// the component-level suite covers only the wiring (`showModal` /
    32	// `close` calls, `close`-event callback, backdrop-click callback,
    33	// restoreFocusRef on close). The real focus-trap assertion lives in
    34	// the Playwright spec.
    35	//
    36	// happy-dom v20 quirk: dispatching a `keydown` Escape on a `<dialog>`
    37	// does NOT close it natively (real Chromium does). To make the
    38	// component-level Esc test meaningful we wire an explicit `keydown`
    39	// listener on the dialog that calls `dialog.close()` when key === Escape.
    40	// In real Chromium this is harmless — the native handler runs first and
    41	// the `dialog.close()` short-circuits because `dialog.open` is already
    42	// false by the time our handler runs.
    43	//
    44	// The drawer is always rendered (never conditionally unmounted); the
    45	// `isOpen` prop drives the imperative `showModal()` / `close()` calls
    46	// inside a `useEffect`. This keeps the underlying `<dialog>` ref stable
    47	// across opens.
    48	//
    49	// CSS for the SHELL (`.drawer`, `.drawer::backdrop`, `.drawer-header`,
    50	// `.drawer-title`, `.drawer-body`, `.drawer-close`) lives in the
    51	// sibling `Drawer.css`. The drawer-body content vocabulary
    52	// (`.drawer-meta`, `.drawer-tool-badge`, `.drawer-conflict-badge`,
    53	// `.raw-preview*`, etc.) lives in `features/sessions/SessionDetail.css`
    54	// alongside its emitter.
    55	import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
    56	import { FocusTrap } from "focus-trap-react";
    57	import "./Drawer.css";
    58	
    59	export type DrawerProps = {
    60	  /** Whether the drawer should be open. Toggling this prop from false to
    61	   *  true calls `dialog.showModal()`; from true to false calls
    62	   *  `dialog.close()` if the dialog is currently open. */
    63	  isOpen: boolean;
    64	  /** Fires when the platform `close` event lands on the dialog (Esc,
    65	   *  programmatic `dialog.close()`, or our backdrop / close-button
    66	   *  shims). The parent owns the `isOpen` boolean and is responsible
    67	   *  for flipping it to false in response. */
    68	  onClose: () => void;
    69	  /** Element to restore focus to after the drawer closes. The native
    70	   *  `<dialog>` already restores focus to the previously-focused
    71	   *  element when `dialog.close()` runs, so this prop is only useful
    72	   *  when the trigger is unmounted/replaced between open and close, or
    73	   *  when the test environment does not implement the platform
    74	   *  restoration (happy-dom). When set, the close handler will call
    75	   *  `restoreFocusRef.current?.focus()` after `onClose`. */
    76	  restoreFocusRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>;
    77	  /** Drawer contents — header, body, copy buttons, etc. The drawer
    78	   *  always renders a close button as the first focusable child, so
    79	   *  consumers do not need to add their own. */
    80	  children: React.ReactNode;
    81	  /** Accessible label for the dialog (announced by screen readers when
    82	   *  the dialog opens). Defaults to "Drawer". */
    83	  ariaLabel?: string;
    84	};
    85	
    86	export function Drawer({
    87	  isOpen,
    88	  onClose,
    89	  restoreFocusRef,
    90	  children,
    91	  ariaLabel,
    92	}: DrawerProps) {
    93	  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDialogElement | null>(null);
    94	
    95	  // Imperatively sync the dialog's open state with the `isOpen` prop.
    96	  // We deliberately do NOT use the React `open` attribute on `<dialog>`:
    97	  // setting it directly bypasses `showModal()` and you lose the modal
    98	  // behaviour (focus trap, backdrop, etc.). The platform requires the
    99	  // imperative `showModal()` / `close()` calls.
   100	  useEffect(() => {
   101	    const dialog = dialogRef.current;
   102	    if (dialog === null) return;
   103	    if (isOpen && !dialog.open) {
   104	      dialog.showModal();
   105	    } else if (!isOpen && dialog.open) {
   106	      dialog.close();
   107	    }
   108	  }, [isOpen]);
   109	
   110	  // Wire the platform `close` event to the parent's `onClose` callback.
   111	  // `close` fires on Esc (Chromium), on `dialog.close()` (any path), and
   112	  // when the close button or backdrop click handlers below call
   113	  // `dialog.close()`. We wire it once on mount.
   114	  useEffect(() => {
   115	    const dialog = dialogRef.current;
   116	    if (dialog === null) return;
   117	    const handler = () => {
   118	      onClose();
   119	      // happy-dom does not implement the platform focus restoration on
   120	      // dialog close; fall back to the explicit ref when the consumer
   121	      // provided one. In real Chromium the native restoration has
   122	      // already run by this point — focusing again is idempotent.
   123	      if (restoreFocusRef?.current) {
   124	        restoreFocusRef.current.focus();
   125	      }
   126	    };
   127	    dialog.addEventListener("close", handler);
   128	    return () => {
   129	      dialog.removeEventListener("close", handler);
   130	    };
   131	  }, [onClose, restoreFocusRef]);
   132	
   133	  // Backdrop click: a click whose target IS the dialog itself (not a
   134	  // descendant) lands on the backdrop. Closing on backdrop click is the
   135	  // standard `<dialog>` UX expectation; the platform does not provide
   136	  // this for free.
   137	  useEffect(() => {
   138	    const dialog = dialogRef.current;
   139	    if (dialog === null) return;
   140	    const handler = (event: MouseEvent) => {
   141	      if (event.target === dialog) {
   142	        dialog.close();
   143	      }
   144	    };
   145	    dialog.addEventListener("click", handler);
   146	    return () => {
   147	      dialog.removeEventListener("click", handler);
   148	    };
   149	  }, []);
   150	
   151	  // Esc fallback: happy-dom does not auto-close on Esc keydown (real
   152	  // Chromium does). We wire an explicit handler so component-level
   153	  // tests pass and so any Chromium quirk where the native handler is
   154	  // disabled (e.g. a captured event upstream) still closes the drawer.
   155	  // The handler is a no-op in real Chromium when the platform has
   156	  // already closed the dialog by the time our React listener fires.
   157	  useEffect(() => {
   158	    const dialog = dialogRef.current;
   159	    if (dialog === null) return;
   160	    const handler = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
   161	      if (event.key === "Escape" && dialog.open) {
   162	        // Prevent the platform default to avoid a duplicate `close`
   163	        // event fire in Chromium where the native handler has not yet
   164	        // run; `dialog.close()` will fire `close` once.
   165	        event.preventDefault();
   166	        dialog.close();
   167	      }
   168	    };
   169	    dialog.addEventListener("keydown", handler);
   170	    return () => {
   171	      dialog.removeEventListener("keydown", handler);
   172	    };
   173	  }, []);
   174	
   175	  const handleCloseClick = () => {
   176	    const dialog = dialogRef.current;
   177	    if (dialog !== null && dialog.open) {
   178	      dialog.close();
   179	    }
   180	  };
   181	
   182	  return (
   183	    <dialog
   184	      ref={dialogRef}
   185	      className="drawer"
   186	      aria-label={ariaLabel ?? "Drawer"}
   187	    >
   188	      {/* `focus-trap-react` is the documented escape-hatch dependency
   189	       *  (per `working/phase-4.md` §Dependency Policy). It activates
   190	       *  when `isOpen` is true and ensures Tab/Shift+Tab cycle
   191	       *  WITHIN the dialog (which native `<dialog>` does NOT do
   192	       *  reliably — see header comment for the failing reproducer).
   193	       *
   194	       *  - `escapeDeactivates: false` so our own keydown handler
   195	       *    above is still the source of truth for Esc-close
   196	       *    (single code path: dialog.close() -> close event ->
   197	       *    onClose). Without this, focus-trap-react would also
   198	       *    trigger deactivation on Esc, double-firing onClose.
   199	       *  - `clickOutsideDeactivates: false` because the dialog
   200	       *    occupies the entire viewport when open and we already
   201	       *    handle backdrop clicks via the dialog's click handler.
   202	       *  - `allowOutsideClick: true` so the backdrop click
   203	       *    handler can fire (focus-trap-react would otherwise
   204	       *    swallow the event). */}
   205	      <FocusTrap
   206	        active={isOpen}
   207	        focusTrapOptions={{
   208	          escapeDeactivates: false,
   209	          clickOutsideDeactivates: false,
   210	          allowOutsideClick: true,
   211	          // Initial focus lands on the close button, the first
   212	          // child of the trap.
   213	          initialFocus: () => {
   214	            const dialog = dialogRef.current;
   215	            return dialog?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
   216	              "button.drawer-close",
   217	            ) ?? false;
   218	          },
   219	          // The trap's container is the inner div; the dialog is
   220	          // the parent. Prevent the trap from focusing the dialog
   221	          // itself.
   222	          fallbackFocus: () => {
   223	            const dialog = dialogRef.current;
   224	            return (
   225	              dialog?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
   226	                "button.drawer-close",
   227	              ) ?? document.body
   228	            );
   229	          },
   230	        }}
   231	      >
   232	        <div className="drawer-trap">
   233	          <button
   234	            type="button"
   235	            className="drawer-close"
   236	            aria-label="Close drawer"
   237	            onClick={handleCloseClick}
   238	          >
   239	            Close
   240	          </button>
   241	          {children}
   242	        </div>
   243	      </FocusTrap>
   244	    </dialog>
   245	  );
   246	}

     2	//
     3	// happy-dom v20 quirks documented during the probe (see Handoff Notes
     4	// in the chunk evidence pack):
     5	//   - `dialog.showModal()` and `dialog.close()` work; `dialog.open`
     6	//     flips correctly.
     7	//   - `dialog.close()` fires the platform `close` event.
     8	//   - `keydown` Escape on the dialog does NOT auto-close (real Chromium
