   172	test("Drawer restores focus to restoreFocusRef.current on close", () => {
   173	  // Build a small harness with a trigger button outside the dialog,
   174	  // open the drawer, close it, then assert the trigger has focus.
   175	  // happy-dom does not implement platform focus restoration; the
   176	  // Drawer's explicit `restoreFocusRef.current?.focus()` call inside
   177	  // the close handler is the path under test.
   178	  function Harness() {
   179	    const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);
   180	    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
   181	    return (
   182	      <>
   183	        <button
   184	          type="button"
   185	          ref={triggerRef}
   186	          onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
   187	          data-testid="trigger"
   188	        >
   189	          Open
   190	        </button>
   191	        <Drawer
   192	          isOpen={open}
   193	          onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
   194	          restoreFocusRef={triggerRef}
   195	        >
   196	          <p>hi</p>
   197	        </Drawer>
   198	      </>
   199	    );
   200	  }
   201	  const { container } = render(<Harness />);
   202	  const trigger = container.querySelector(
   203	    '[data-testid="trigger"]',
   204	  ) as HTMLButtonElement;
   205	  trigger.focus();
   206	  // Click the trigger to open the drawer.
   207	  act(() => {
   208	    trigger.click();
   209	  });
   210	  const dialog = container.querySelector("dialog") as HTMLDialogElement;
   211	  expect(dialog.open).toBe(true);
   212	  // Programmatic close to fire the close event and the restoration.
   213	  act(() => {
   214	    dialog.close();
   215	  });
   216	  expect(dialog.open).toBe(false);
   217	  expect(document.activeElement).toBe(trigger);
   218	});

     1	// Inspection-surface browser e2e.
     2	//
     3	// Drives the real React app against the real Rust backend through the
     4	// Vite dev proxy. Covers the full inspection workflow (render, import,
     5	// rescan, raw payload retrieval). The companion Rust-level typed-client
     6	// coverage lives at `tests/e2e/tests/inspection_surface.rs` (backend
     7	// HTTP stack directly, no browser).
     8	//
     9	// Topology during this spec:
    10	//   chromium -> http://127.0.0.1:4100 (Vite dev server, webServer hook)
    11	//             -> proxies /api/v1 + /health to http://127.0.0.1:4000
    12	//             -> Rust backend spawned by `startBackend(...)` in beforeAll
    13	//
    14	// `test.describe.serial` + `workers: 1` guarantees we don't double-bind
    15	// port 4000 across test files. The fixture is seeded into the
    16	// harness-owned temp dir BEFORE the backend starts, so the first
    17	// `/api/v1/source-sessions` response already contains it.

   502	    // After the pulse settles (≤ 2 s safety timer + a small slack

   519	    await expect(ariaCurrentRow).toBeVisible({ timeout: 5_000 });
   520	    await expect(ariaCurrentRow).toContainText(FIXTURE_SESSION_KEY);

 test("scan-errors error path: Retry button refetches all three panels", async () => {
   // Per the M3 partial-fetch-failure contract, every panel-error
   // surface must offer a Retry/refetch affordance. The scan-errors
   // alert is the third such surface (the source/stored alerts inside
   // SessionsView already have one). After the initial 500 the user
   // clicks Retry; the click invokes `refetchAll`, which fires three
   // fresh GETs (source + stored + scan-errors). On the second pass
   // we serve the scan-errors fixture so the alert is replaced by the
   // ScanErrorsCallout — proving the refetch landed.
   let scanErrorsCallCount = 0;
   const fetchMock = mock(
     async (input: Request | string | URL): Promise<Response> => {
       const url = urlOf(input);
       if (url === SOURCE_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse(SOURCE_FIXTURE);
       if (url === STORED_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse(STORED_FIXTURE);
       if (url === SCAN_ERRORS_PATH) {
         scanErrorsCallCount += 1;
         if (scanErrorsCallCount === 1) {
           return new Response("boom", {
             status: 500,
             headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain" },
           });
         }
         return jsonResponse(SCAN_ERRORS_FIXTURE);
       }
       return new Response(`unexpected url ${url}`, { status: 404 });
     },
   );
   globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
 
   const { container } = render(<App />);
 
   // Wait for the initial render: the unified table renders + the
   // scan-errors alert appears.
   await screen.findByText("claude_code:fixture-abc");
   await waitFor(() => {
     const alerts = Array.from(
       container.querySelectorAll('[role="alert"]'),
     );
     const scanAlert = alerts.find((el) =>
       el.textContent?.startsWith("Failed to load scan errors"),
     );
     expect(scanAlert).not.toBeUndefined();
   });
   // Three initial GETs (source + stored + scan-errors).
   const initialCallCount = fetchMock.mock.calls.length;
   expect(initialCallCount).toBe(3);
 
   // The Retry button lives inside the scan-errors alert (NOT inside
   // SessionsView's per-section banners, which fire only when source
   // or stored fail).
   const alerts = Array.from(
     container.querySelectorAll('[role="alert"]'),
   );
   const scanAlert = alerts.find((el) =>
     el.textContent?.startsWith("Failed to load scan errors"),
   );
   expect(scanAlert).not.toBeUndefined();
   const retryButton = scanAlert!.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
     "button",
   );
   expect(retryButton).not.toBeNull();
   expect(retryButton!.textContent).toBe("Retry");
 
   // Click Retry -> refetchAll() fires three fresh GETs.
   await act(async () => {
     retryButton!.click();

 // ---------- Phase 4 Milestone 5 (Chunk F) additions ----------
 //
 // (a) 500-row layout — pagination renders 50 rows per page; the
 //     pagination caption reads "Page 1 of 10".
 // (b) Pagination-cross-page CLICK-TIME INTERSECTION REGRESSION —
 //     a row selected on page 1 that gets pruned by a rescan must
 //     NOT ship in the import POST when the user is viewing page 2.
 //     Mirrors the F2 + M3 microtask choreography. The temp-revert
 //     exercise is described in the chunk handoff: temporarily
 //     scoping handleImport's eligible set to `pageRows + isImportable`
 //     instead of `filteredRows + isImportable` makes this test fail
 //     (POST body becomes empty, since the stale key was on page 1
 //     and we're now on page 2). Restoring filteredRows-scoped
 //     derivation makes it pass.
 // (c) Cross-page bulk-select — the header checkbox in SessionsTable
 //     now operates on the full FILTER window (M3 wiring intact under
 //     pagination); selecting all on page 1 should put cross-page
 //     keys into the POST body too.
 // (d) Page-reset on filter change — after paging through to page 3,
 //     applying any filter resets pageIndex to 0.
 // (e) Page-reset RECOMPUTE on pageSize change — keeps the first
 //     visible row visible (page 2 with size 50 -> rows 50-99;
 //     change to size 100 -> page 1 -> rows 0-99 -> still includes
 //     row 50).
 // (f) Toast queue tests — rescan success / import success / rescan
 //     error with Retry that succeeds on second attempt.
 // (g) Last-rescan caption — initially "—"; after rescan success
 //     renders the relative-time form; after rescan ERROR, the
 //     caption does NOT update.
 
 // Helper: build N synthetic SourceSessionView rows. Status alternates
 // every 1 row so all rows are importable (important for the cross-page
 // regression and the bulk-select test).
 function manySource(n: number): SourceSessionView[] {
   const rows: SourceSessionView[] = [];
   for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
     rows.push({
       session_key: `claude_code:row-${i.toString().padStart(4, "0")}`,
       tool: "claude_code",
       source_session_id: `row-${i.toString().padStart(4, "0")}`,
       source_path: `/srv/r${i}.jsonl`,
       source_fingerprint: `fp-r-${i}`,
       // Spaced timestamps so the descending-by-source_updated_at
       // default sort produces a deterministic order: row-0 newest.
       created_at: `2026-04-22T00:00:00Z`,
       source_updated_at: `2026-04-22T00:${(99 - Math.floor(i / 60))
         .toString()
         .padStart(2, "0")}:${(60 - (i % 60))
         .toString()
         .padStart(2, "0")}Z`,
       project_path: "/p/x",
       title: `Row ${i}`,
       has_subagent_sidecars: false,
       status: "not_stored",
       session_uid: null,
       stored_ingested_at: null,
     });
   }

 test("M1a: URL-on-mount with ?session=<rowKey> pre-selects + matched row carries data-deep-link='true'", async () => {
   resetUrl();
   // Pre-seed the URL BEFORE mounting App. The hook reads
   // URLSearchParams on mount.

     makeM1aFetchMock() as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;

   // The matched row must render with aria-current + data-deep-link
   // for the duration of the pulse.

     const matched = container.querySelector(
       'tbody tr[aria-current="true"]',
     );
     expect(matched).not.toBeNull();

   const matched = container.querySelector(
     'tbody tr[aria-current="true"]',
   );
   // The row's text content should include the matching session key.
   expect(matched?.textContent?.includes("claude_code:m1a-1")).toBe(true);
   // data-deep-link is the M1a pulse attribute. Click-driven
   // selection NEVER carries it; URL-driven mount does.
   expect(matched?.getAttribute("data-deep-link")).toBe("true");
   // Right pane state is "ready-placeholder" once the row merged in.
   await waitFor(() => {
     const article = container.querySelector("article.session-pane");
     expect(article?.getAttribute("data-state")).toBe("ready-placeholder");
   });
 });

  1234	//     rather than a rescan. Two not_stored rows are loaded; the user
  1235	//     selects both via per-row checkboxes; the user then sets
  1236	//     `tool=codex` (which hides the claude_code row). Without the
  1237	//     filter-window extension to the click-time intersection, the
  1238	//     import POST would still ship both selected keys (the raw
  1239	//     `selected` set is unchanged across filter mutations per spec).
  1240	//     With the extension, the POST body must contain only the visible
  1241	//     codex key.
  1242	// (b) Multi-filter integration — render with mixed fixtures, apply
  1243	//     three filter axes, assert exactly the matching subset renders.
  1244	// (c) Four empty-state branches — distinct copy + working affordances.
  1245	
  1246	test("M3: filter mutation + click-time intersection — POST contains only the visible-after-filter key", async () => {
  1247	  // Mixed-tool fixture: one claude_code + one codex; both not_stored
  1248	  // (so both render checkboxes). After the user selects both, applying
  1249	  // a `tool=codex` filter must hide the claude_code row from the POST.
  1250	  const SOURCE: SourceSessionView[] = [
  1251	    {
  1252	      session_key: "claude_code:filter-A",
  1253	      tool: "claude_code",
  1254	      source_session_id: "filter-A",
  1255	      source_path: "/tmp/fixture/a.jsonl",
  1256	      source_fingerprint: "fp-a",
  1257	      created_at: "2026-04-22T00:00:00Z",
  1258	      source_updated_at: "2026-04-22T00:00:00Z",
  1259	      project_path: "/tmp/fixture",
  1260	      title: "Filter A (claude)",
  1261	      has_subagent_sidecars: false,
  1262	      status: "not_stored",
  1263	      session_uid: null,
  1264	      stored_ingested_at: null,
  1265	    },
  1266	    {
  1267	      session_key: "codex:filter-B",
  1268	      tool: "codex",
  1269	      source_session_id: "filter-B",
  1270	      source_path: "/tmp/fixture/b.jsonl",
  1271	      source_fingerprint: "fp-b",
  1272	      created_at: "2026-04-22T00:00:00Z",
  1273	      source_updated_at: "2026-04-22T00:00:00Z",
  1274	      project_path: "/tmp/fixture",
  1275	      title: "Filter B (codex)",
  1276	      has_subagent_sidecars: false,
  1277	      status: "not_stored",
  1278	      session_uid: null,
  1279	      stored_ingested_at: null,
  1280	    },
  1281	  ];
  1282	
  1283	  const fetchMock = mock(
  1284	    async (
  1285	      input: Request | string | URL,
  1286	      init?: RequestInit,
  1287	    ): Promise<Response> => {
  1288	      const url = urlOf(input);
  1289	      const method = init?.method ?? "GET";
  1290	      if (method === "POST" && url === IMPORT_PATH) {
  1291	        return jsonResponse(IMPORT_FIXTURE);
  1292	      }
  1293	      if (method === "GET" && url === SOURCE_SESSIONS_PATH) {
  1294	        return jsonResponse(SOURCE);
  1295	      }
  1296	      if (method === "GET" && url === STORED_SESSIONS_PATH) {
  1297	        return jsonResponse([]);
  1298	      }
  1299	      if (method === "GET" && url === SCAN_ERRORS_PATH) {
  1300	        return jsonResponse([]);
  1301	      }
  1302	      return new Response(`unexpected ${method} ${url}`, { status: 404 });
  1303	    },
  1304	  );
  1305	  globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
  1306	
  1307	  const { container } = render(<App />);
  1308	
  1309	  // Wait for both rows to render.
  1310	  await screen.findByText("claude_code:filter-A");
  1311	  await screen.findByText("codex:filter-B");
  1312	
  1313	  // Select both per-row checkboxes.
  1314	  const checkboxA = container.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(
  1315	    'input[type="checkbox"][aria-label="Select claude_code:filter-A"]',
  1316	  );
  1317	  const checkboxB = container.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(
  1318	    'input[type="checkbox"][aria-label="Select codex:filter-B"]',
  1319	  );
  1320	  expect(checkboxA).not.toBeNull();
  1321	  expect(checkboxB).not.toBeNull();
  1322	  await act(async () => {
  1323	    checkboxA?.click();
  1324	  });
  1325	  await act(async () => {
  1326	    checkboxB?.click();
  1327	  });
  1328	
  1329	  // Sanity: import button reads count=2 (two visible importable +
  1330	  // two selected). zero hidden by filter at this point.
  1331	  const importButton = container.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
  1332	    ".action-bar button:nth-of-type(2)",
  1333	  );
  1334	  expect(importButton?.textContent).toBe("Import selected (2)");
  1335	
  1336	  // Apply tool=codex filter via the chip; the claude_code row drops
  1337	  // out of the FILTER window. The selection set is unchanged
  1338	  // (per spec: filter changes do not clear raw selection); the
  1339	  // action-bar count drops to 1 and the +1 hidden by filters caption
  1340	  // appears.
  1341	  const codexChip = Array.from(
  1342	    container.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>("button.chip"),
  1343	  ).find((el) => el.textContent === "Codex");
  1344	  expect(codexChip).not.toBeUndefined();
  1345	  await act(async () => {
  1346	    codexChip!.click();
  1347	  });
  1348	
  1349	  // Action-bar count is now 1 (only codex:filter-B is in the filter
  1350	  // window's importable set); +1 hidden by filters caption is in DOM.
  1351	  expect(importButton?.textContent).toBe("Import selected (1)");
  1352	  const hiddenCaption = container.querySelector(".action-bar-hidden-caption");
  1353	  expect(hiddenCaption?.textContent).toBe("+1 hidden by filters");
  1354	
  1355	  // Click Import.
  1356	  await act(async () => {
  1357	    importButton?.click();
  1358	  });
  1359	  await waitFor(() => {
  1360	    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(7); // 3 initial + 1 POST + 3 refetch
  1361	  });
  1362	
  1363	  // The POST body MUST contain ONLY the codex key — the click-time
  1364	  // intersection drops the hidden claude_code key.
  1365	  const importCalls = fetchMock.mock.calls.filter((args) => {
  1366	    const init = args[1] as RequestInit | undefined;
  1367	    const url = urlOf(args[0] as Request | string | URL);
  1368	    return (init?.method ?? "GET") === "POST" && url === IMPORT_PATH;
  1369	  });
  1370	  expect(importCalls.length).toBe(1);
  1371	  const body = importCalls[0]?.[1]?.body as string;
  1372	  expect(body).toBe(
  1373	    JSON.stringify({ session_keys: ["codex:filter-B"] }),
  1374	  );
  1375	  // Defensive substring assertion: the hidden key cannot appear under
  1376	  // any future serialization.
  1377	  expect(body.includes("claude_code:filter-A")).toBe(false);
  1378	});

  1587	test("M3 empty state — Nothing to import in current filter (Show importable only flips the boolean)", async () => {
  1588	  // Per `working/phase-4.md` §Filter, Sort, Search → Empty States
  1589	  // (lines 381–386), the "Nothing to import in the current filter"
  1590	  // empty state fires when matching rows EXIST (filteredRows > 0)
  1591	  // but every visible row is non-importable (`up_to_date` or
  1592	  // `source_missing`). The empty-state copy + a "Show importable
  1593	  // only" affordance render alongside the table (the table is
  1594	  // informative; rows describe what the user CAN see). Clicking
  1595	  // the affordance flips `importableOnly` to `true`.
  1596	  //
  1597	  // Fixture: one `up_to_date` row (joined source ⊕ stored) and zero
  1598	  // `not_stored` / `outdated` rows. No filter is active on mount,
  1599	  // so the filter pipeline returns the same one row; `isImportable`
  1600	  // is false for it, so the branch fires.
  1601	  const SOURCE: SourceSessionView[] = [
  1602	    {
  1603	      session_key: "claude_code:up-1",
  1604	      tool: "claude_code",
  1605	      source_session_id: "up-1",
  1606	      source_path: "/srv/u.jsonl",
  1607	      source_fingerprint: "fp-u",
  1608	      created_at: null,
  1609	      source_updated_at: null,
  1610	      project_path: null,
  1611	      title: "Up-to-date 1",
  1612	      has_subagent_sidecars: false,
  1613	      status: "up_to_date",
  1614	      session_uid: "uid-u",
  1615	      stored_ingested_at: "2026-04-22T00:00:00Z",
  1616	    },
  1617	  ];
  1618	  const STORED: StoredSessionView[] = [
  1619	    {
  1620	      status: "up_to_date",
  1621	      session_uid: "uid-u",
  1622	      tool: "claude_code",
  1623	      source_session_id: "up-1",
  1624	      source_path: "/srv/u.jsonl",
  1625	      source_fingerprint: "fp-u",
  1626	      raw_ref: "raw/uid-u.ndjson",
  1627	      created_at: null,
  1628	      source_updated_at: null,
  1629	      ingested_at: "2026-04-22T00:00:00Z",
  1630	      project_path: null,
  1631	      title: "Up-to-date 1",
  1632	      has_subagent_sidecars: false,
  1633	    },
  1634	  ];
  1635	  const fetchMock = mock(
  1636	    async (input: Request | string | URL): Promise<Response> => {
  1637	      const url = urlOf(input);
  1638	      if (url === SOURCE_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse(SOURCE);
  1639	      if (url === STORED_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse(STORED);
  1640	      if (url === SCAN_ERRORS_PATH) return jsonResponse([]);
  1641	      return new Response(`unexpected url ${url}`, { status: 404 });
  1642	    },
  1643	  );
  1644	  globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
  1645	  const { container } = render(<App />);
  1646	  await screen.findByText("claude_code:up-1");
  1647	  // The spec-mandated empty-state copy renders.
  1648	  await waitFor(() => {
  1649	    expect(
  1650	      container.textContent?.includes(
  1651	        "Nothing to import in the current filter.",
  1652	      ),
  1653	    ).toBe(true);
  1654	  });
  1655	  // The empty-state affordance is a button that says "Show
  1656	  // importable only" and lives inside the .empty block (NOT to be
  1657	  // confused with the SessionFilters chip of the same aria-label,
  1658	  // which always renders in the filter bar).
  1659	  const emptyBlock = container.querySelector("div.empty");
  1660	  expect(emptyBlock).not.toBeNull();
  1661	  const showOnlyButton = Array.from(
  1662	    emptyBlock!.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>("button"),
  1663	  ).find((b) => b.textContent === "Show importable only");
  1664	  expect(showOnlyButton).not.toBeUndefined();
  1665	  // Capture the importable-only chip in the filter bar to verify
  1666	  // its aria-pressed state flips after the affordance click. The
  1667	  // chip owns the live boolean; the empty-state button merely calls
  1668	  // setImportableOnly(true) on its behalf.
  1669	  const importableChip = container.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
  1670	    'button.chip[aria-label="Show importable only"]',
  1671	  );
  1672	  expect(importableChip).not.toBeNull();
  1673	  expect(importableChip!.getAttribute("aria-pressed")).toBe("false");
  1674	  // Click the empty-state affordance -> flips importableOnly to true.
  1675	  await act(async () => {
  1676	    showOnlyButton!.click();
  1677	  });
  1678	  await waitFor(() => {
  1679	    const refreshedChip = container.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
  1680	      'button.chip[aria-label="Show importable only"]',
  1681	    );
  1682	    expect(refreshedChip!.getAttribute("aria-pressed")).toBe("true");
  1683	  });
  1684	  // After the toggle, importableOnly === true narrows the effective
  1685	  // status set to ["not_stored", "outdated"], dropping the lone
  1686	  // up_to_date row — so we now hit the "No sessions match the
  1687	  // current filter." branch (a filter is active).
  1688	  await waitFor(() => {
  1689	    expect(
  1690	      container.textContent?.includes(
  1691	        "No sessions match the current filter.",
  1692	      ),
  1693	    ).toBe(true);
  1694	  });
  1695	});

  1697	test("M3 empty state — Partial fetch failure (source 500) preserves stored rows", async () => {
  1698	  // Mirrors the M2 per-panel error isolation test, but verifies M3
  1699	  // didn't regress the behavior. Source-fetch fails; stored fetch
  1700	  // succeeds with one stored_only row; the unified table still
  1701	  // renders that row + the per-section banner.
  1702	  const STORED: StoredSessionView[] = [
  1703	    {

  1714	      project_path: null,
  1715	      title: "Survived",
  1716	      has_subagent_sidecars: false,
  1717	    },
  1718	  ];
  1719	  const fetchMock = mock(
  1720	    async (input: Request | string | URL): Promise<Response> => {
  1721	      const url = urlOf(input);
  1722	      if (url === SOURCE_SESSIONS_PATH) {
  1723	        return new Response("boom", {
  1724	          status: 500,
  1725	          headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain" },
  1726	        });
  1727	      }
  1728	      if (url === STORED_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse(STORED);
  1729	      if (url === SCAN_ERRORS_PATH) return jsonResponse([]);
  1730	      return new Response(`unexpected url ${url}`, { status: 404 });
  1731	    },
  1732	  );
  1733	  globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
  1734	  const { container } = render(<App />);
  1735	  // M1b: the stored UID is no longer in the inline DOM (the Stored
  1736	  // Copy column was dropped). The synthesized `stored:<uid>` rowKey
  1737	  // surfaces inside the Title cell stack instead.
  1738	  await screen.findByText("stored:uid-survived");
  1739	  // The per-section banner is present.
  1740	  const alerts = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll('[role="alert"]'));
  1741	  const sourceAlert = alerts.find((el) =>
  1742	    el.textContent?.startsWith("Failed to load source sessions"),
  1743	  );
  1744	  expect(sourceAlert).not.toBeUndefined();
  1745	  // The stored-only survivor row is in the DOM (i.e. the table renders).
  1746	  expect(container.textContent?.includes("stored:uid-survived")).toBe(true);
  1747	});

  1769	// ---------- Phase 4 Milestone 5 (Chunk F) additions ----------
  1770	//
  1771	// (a) 500-row layout — pagination renders 50 rows per page; the
  1772	//     pagination caption reads "Page 1 of 10".
  1773	// (b) Pagination-cross-page CLICK-TIME INTERSECTION REGRESSION —
  1774	//     a row selected on page 1 that gets pruned by a rescan must
  1775	//     NOT ship in the import POST when the user is viewing page 2.
  1776	//     Mirrors the F2 + M3 microtask choreography. The temp-revert
  1777	//     exercise is described in the chunk handoff: temporarily
  1778	//     scoping handleImport's eligible set to `pageRows + isImportable`
  1779	//     instead of `filteredRows + isImportable` makes this test fail
  1780	//     (POST body becomes empty, since the stale key was on page 1
  1781	//     and we're now on page 2). Restoring filteredRows-scoped
  1782	//     derivation makes it pass.
  1783	// (c) Cross-page bulk-select — the header checkbox in SessionsTable
  1784	//     now operates on the full FILTER window (M3 wiring intact under
  1785	//     pagination); selecting all on page 1 should put cross-page
  1786	//     keys into the POST body too.
  1787	// (d) Page-reset on filter change — after paging through to page 3,
  1788	//     applying any filter resets pageIndex to 0.
  1789	// (e) Page-reset RECOMPUTE on pageSize change — keeps the first
  1790	//     visible row visible (page 2 with size 50 -> rows 50-99;
  1791	//     change to size 100 -> page 1 -> rows 0-99 -> still includes
  1792	//     row 50).
  1793	// (f) Toast queue tests — rescan success / import success / rescan
  1794	//     error with Retry that succeeds on second attempt.
  1795	// (g) Last-rescan caption — initially "—"; after rescan success
  1796	//     renders the relative-time form; after rescan ERROR, the
  1797	//     caption does NOT update.
  1798	
  1799	// Helper: build N synthetic SourceSessionView rows. Status alternates
  1800	// every 1 row so all rows are importable (important for the cross-page
  1801	// regression and the bulk-select test).
  1802	function manySource(n: number): SourceSessionView[] {
  1803	  const rows: SourceSessionView[] = [];
  1804	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
  1805	    rows.push({
  1806	      session_key: `claude_code:row-${i.toString().padStart(4, "0")}`,
  1807	      tool: "claude_code",
  1808	      source_session_id: `row-${i.toString().padStart(4, "0")}`,
  1809	      source_path: `/srv/r${i}.jsonl`,
  1810	      source_fingerprint: `fp-r-${i}`,
  1811	      // Spaced timestamps so the descending-by-source_updated_at
  1812	      // default sort produces a deterministic order: row-0 newest.
  1813	      created_at: `2026-04-22T00:00:00Z`,
  1814	      source_updated_at: `2026-04-22T00:${(99 - Math.floor(i / 60))
  1815	        .toString()
  1816	        .padStart(2, "0")}:${(60 - (i % 60))
  1817	        .toString()
  1818	        .padStart(2, "0")}Z`,
  1819	      project_path: "/p/x",
  1820	      title: `Row ${i}`,
  1821	      has_subagent_sidecars: false,
  1822	      status: "not_stored",
  1823	      session_uid: null,
  1824	      stored_ingested_at: null,
  1825	    });
  1826	  }
  1827	  return rows;
  1828	}
  1829

  1834	      const url = urlOf(input);
  1835	      if (url === SOURCE_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse(SOURCE);
  1836	      if (url === STORED_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse([]);
  1837	      if (url === SCAN_ERRORS_PATH) return jsonResponse([]);
  1838	      return new Response(`unexpected url ${url}`, { status: 404 });
  1839	    },
  1840	  );
  1841	  globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
  1842	  const { container } = render(<App />);
  1843	  // Wait for one of the high-id rows we know lands on the deterministic

  1862	test("M5: pagination-cross-page click-time intersection — pruned page-1 key drops; surviving page-1 keys stay (cross-page accumulation)", async () => {
  1863	  // Setup: 51 importable rows. Default pageSize is 50, so:
  1864	  //   - page 1 has 50 rows (row-0000 .. row-0049)
  1865	  //   - page 2 has 1 row (row-0050)
  1866	  // The user selects two keys on page 1 (row-0000 and row-0010), then
  1867	  // pages to page 2. A rescan fires that prunes row-0010 from the
  1868	  // source list. The test then triggers the import via the F2 + M3
  1869	  // microtask choreography (text-node setter hook + queueMicrotask
  1870	  // scheduled click during a setSelected commit). The POST body must
  1871	  // contain row-0000 (still present in the filter window) but NOT
  1872	  // row-0010 (pruned by the rescan).
  1873	  //
  1874	  // The choreography: install the setter hook on the Import button's
  1875	  // text node; flip IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT to false; click Rescan
  1876	  // (without `act()`); when React commits "Import selected (1)" the
  1877	  // setter fires, microtask-schedules a click on Import. The click
  1878	  // fires before React's reconciliation `useEffect` flushes, so
  1879	  // `selected` still contains row-0010. The handler's click-time
  1880	  // intersection (over filteredRows + isImportable) drops row-0010.
  1881	  const INITIAL = manySource(51); // 51 rows; pages 1 + 2
  1882	  // After the rescan, row-0010 is pruned (returns 50 rows excluding
  1883	  // row-0010).
  1884	  const AFTER_RESCAN = INITIAL.filter(
  1885	    (r) => r.session_key !== "claude_code:row-0010",
  1886	  );
  1887	
  1888	  let rescanned = false;
  1889	  const fetchMock = mock(
  1890	    async (
  1891	      input: Request | string | URL,
  1892	      init?: RequestInit,
  1893	    ): Promise<Response> => {
  1894	      const url = urlOf(input);
  1895	      const method = init?.method ?? "GET";
  1896	      if (method === "POST" && url === RESCAN_PATH) {
  1897	        rescanned = true;
  1898	        return jsonResponse(RESCAN_FIXTURE);
  1899	      }
  1900	      if (method === "POST" && url === IMPORT_PATH) {
  1901	        return jsonResponse(IMPORT_FIXTURE);
  1902	      }
  1903	      if (method === "GET" && url === SOURCE_SESSIONS_PATH) {
  1904	        return jsonResponse(rescanned ? AFTER_RESCAN : INITIAL);
  1905	      }
  1906	      if (method === "GET" && url === STORED_SESSIONS_PATH) {
  1907	        return jsonResponse([]);
  1908	      }
  1909	      if (method === "GET" && url === SCAN_ERRORS_PATH) {
  1910	        return jsonResponse([]);
  1911	      }
  1912	      return new Response(`unexpected ${method} ${url}`, { status: 404 });
  1913	    },
  1914	  );
  1915	  globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
  1916	  const { container } = render(<App />);
  1917	
  1918	  // Wait for page 1 to settle.
  1919	  await screen.findByText("claude_code:row-0000");
  1920	
  1921	  // Select row-0000 and row-0010 (both on page 1 under default sort).
  1922	  const cb0000 = container.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(
  1923	    'input[type="checkbox"][aria-label="Select claude_code:row-0000"]',
  1924	  );
  1925	  const cb0010 = container.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>(
  1926	    'input[type="checkbox"][aria-label="Select claude_code:row-0010"]',
  1927	  );
  1928	  expect(cb0000).not.toBeNull();
  1929	  expect(cb0010).not.toBeNull();
  1930	  await act(async () => {
  1931	    cb0000?.click();
  1932	  });
  1933	  await act(async () => {
  1934	    cb0010?.click();
  1935	  });
  1936	
  1937	  // Sanity: action-bar count is 2.
  1938	  const importButton = container.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
  1939	    ".action-bar button:nth-of-type(2)",
  1940	  );
  1941	  expect(importButton?.textContent).toBe("Import selected (2)");
  1942	
  1943	  // Page to page 2 by clicking Next.
  1944	  const nextButton = container.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
  1945	    'button[aria-label="Next page"]',
  1946	  );
  1947	  expect(nextButton).not.toBeNull();
  1948	  await act(async () => {
  1949	    nextButton!.click();
  1950	  });
  1951	
  1952	  // Confirm we're on page 2: row-0050 is visible, row-0000 is NOT
  1953	  // visible in the table body (it's still in `filteredRows` but
  1954	  // pagination has windowed it off).
  1955	  await waitFor(() => {
  1956	    expect(
  1957	      container.textContent?.includes("claude_code:row-0050"),
  1958	    ).toBe(true);
  1959	  });
  1960	  // Sanity: the action-bar count is STILL 2 because selection is
  1961	  // filter-window scoped, not page-window scoped.
  1962	  expect(importButton?.textContent).toBe("Import selected (2)");
  1963	
  1964	  // Install the text-node setter hook on the Import button. When
  1965	  // React commits the post-rescan "Import selected (1)" label
  1966	  // (because row-0010 was pruned and the reconciliation useEffect
  1967	  // dropped it from `selected`), microtask-schedule a click on
  1968	  // Import. The click fires BEFORE handleImport's commit-time
  1969	  // re-derivation reads the post-prune state — but the click-time
  1970	  // intersection in `handleImport` defends against that race: the
  1971	  // FRESH re-derivation inside the handler reads the latest
  1972	  // sourceState/storedState/filters, so row-0010 is dropped
  1973	  // regardless of timing.
  1974	  let clicked = false;
  1975	  const findDescriptor = (
  1976	    node: object,
  1977	    prop: string,
  1978	  ): PropertyDescriptor | undefined => {
  1979	    let p: object | null = Object.getPrototypeOf(node);
  1980	    while (p !== null) {
  1981	      const d = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(p, prop);
  1982	      if (d) return d;
  1983	      p = Object.getPrototypeOf(p);
  1984	    }
  1985	    return undefined;
  1986	  };
  1987	  const textNode = importButton!.firstChild;
  1988	  if (textNode) {
  1989	    const descriptor = findDescriptor(textNode, "data");
  1990	    if (descriptor?.set && descriptor.get) {
  1991	      const { set: originalSet, get: originalGet } = descriptor;
  1992	      Object.defineProperty(textNode, "data", {
  1993	        configurable: true,
  1994	        get() {
  1995	          return originalGet.call(this);
  1996	        },
  1997	        set(value: string) {
  1998	          originalSet.call(this, value);
  1999	          if (!clicked && value === "Import selected (1)") {
  2000	            clicked = true;
  2001	            queueMicrotask(() => {
  2002	              importButton!.click();
  2003	            });
  2004	          }
  2005	        },
  2006	      });
  2007	    }
  2008	  }
  2009	
  2010	  // Click Rescan WITHOUT `act()` so React's passive-effect macrotask
  2011	  // does not run inside our click. Same pattern as the F2 + M3
  2012	  // regression tests.
  2013	  const originalActEnv = (globalThis as unknown as {
  2014	    IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT?: boolean;
  2015	  }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT;
  2016	  (globalThis as unknown as {
  2017	    IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT: boolean;
  2018	  }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = false;
  2019	  const rescanButton = container.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(
  2020	    ".action-bar button:nth-of-type(1)",
  2021	  );
  2022	  rescanButton?.click();
  2023	
  2024	  // Wait for the microtask-scheduled Import click to land.
  2025	  await waitFor(() => {
  2026	    expect(clicked).toBe(true);
  2027	  });
  2028	
  2029	  (globalThis as unknown as {
  2030	    IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT: boolean | undefined;
  2031	  }).IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = originalActEnv;
  2032	
  2033	  // Wait for the import POST + its refetch cycle.
  2034	  // 3 initial GETs + 1 rescan POST + 3 refetch GETs + 1 import POST
  2035	  // + 3 post-import refetch GETs = 11.
  2036	  await waitFor(() => {
  2037	    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(11);
  2038	  });
  2039	
  2040	  // Exactly one Import POST. Body MUST include row-0000 (still in the
  2041	  // filter window after the rescan). MUST NOT include row-0010 (pruned).
  2042	  const importCalls = fetchMock.mock.calls.filter((args) => {
  2043	    const init = args[1] as RequestInit | undefined;
  2044	    const url = urlOf(args[0] as Request | string | URL);
  2045	    return (init?.method ?? "GET") === "POST" && url === IMPORT_PATH;
  2046	  });
  2047	  expect(importCalls.length).toBe(1);
  2048	  const body = importCalls[0]?.[1]?.body as string;
  2049	  expect(body.includes("claude_code:row-0000")).toBe(true);
  2050	  expect(body.includes("claude_code:row-0010")).toBe(false);
  2051	});
  2052	
  2053	test("M5: cross-page bulk-select — header checkbox selects ALL importable rows across all pages", async () => {

  2177	test("M5: page-reset RECOMPUTE on pageSize change — keeps the first visible row visible", async () => {
  2178	  // With 500 rows + pageSize 50 + pageIndex 2, the user sees rows 100-149.
  2179	  // Switching to pageSize 100 must put pageIndex at 1 (rows 100-199 —
  2180	  // row 100 still visible).
  2181	  const SOURCE = manySource(500);
  2182	  const fetchMock = mock(
  2183	    async (input: Request | string | URL): Promise<Response> => {
  2184	      const url = urlOf(input);
  2185	      if (url === SOURCE_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse(SOURCE);
  2186	      if (url === STORED_SESSIONS_PATH) return jsonResponse([]);
  2187	      if (url === SCAN_ERRORS_PATH) return jsonResponse([]);
  2188	      return new Response(`unexpected url ${url}`, { status: 404 });
  2189	    },
  2190	  );
  2191	  globalThis.fetch = fetchMock as unknown as typeof globalThis.fetch;
  2192	  const { container } = render(<App />);
  2193	  await screen.findByText("claude_code:row-0000");
  2194	
  2195	  // Page to pageIndex=2 (Page 3 of 10).

     1	// Drawer body for the unified inspection list.
     2	//
     3	// Renders every field on the merged `SessionRow` so a user can inspect
     4	// a single session without leaving the page. Per
     5	// `working/phase-4.md` §Session Detail Drawer the body contains:
     6	//
     7	//   - Title header with the tool badge, status pill, and (when
     8	//     `statusConflict`) a small "Conflict" badge alongside.
     9	//   - Metadata `<dl>` listing every SessionRow field with a labeled
    10	//     `<dt>` + `<dd>`. Timestamp fields render the absolute ISO value
    11	//     PLUS a relative form via `relativeTimeFrom(now, value)`. The
    12	//     `createdAt` and `sourceUpdatedAt` fields are annotated
    13	//     "(source clock)" because the backend records them from the
    14	//     source machine and they may be skewed; `ingestedAt` is annotated
    15	//     "(backend clock)" because it is monotonic.
    16	//   - Source path with a "Last seen source path:" label swap when
    17	//     `sourcePathIsStale` is true (the file is no longer
    18	//     discoverable). A copy-to-clipboard button sits next to it; the
    19	//     fallback when `navigator.clipboard` is unavailable selects the
    20	//     path text so the user can copy it manually with Ctrl+C.
    21	//   - "View raw" anchor (only rendered for stored sessions, i.e.
    22	//     `storedSessionUid !== null`) opens the existing
    23	//     `/api/v1/sessions/:uid/raw` endpoint in a new tab.
    24	//   - Raw preview block (stored sessions only). The block streams the
    25	//     `/api/v1/sessions/:uid/raw` endpoint via `streamSessionRaw` +
    26	//     `consumeRawPreview` and renders the structured `RawPreviewState`
    27	//     (loading / success / error / non_2xx). M4 Chunk E2 added the
    28	//     live block — the placeholder from E1 is gone.
    29	//
    30	// SessionDetail is presentational EXCEPT for the raw-preview block,
    31	// which owns its own fetch lifecycle (one AbortController per
    32	// SessionDetail mount, restarted whenever the row changes — e.g. the
    33	// user closes the drawer and opens a different row). The parent
    34	// (`SessionsView`) owns the open-state, the row lookup, and the
    35	// "now" timestamp pinning. All field reads are done at render time
    36	// so the drawer always shows whatever the merged row carries when
    37	// the parent picks a key.
    38	//
    39	// As of M6 (Chunk G) the status pill is rendered inline (the dedicated
    40	// `StatusBadge` component was retired). The transform —
    41	// `variant = status.replace(/_/g, "-")` for the CSS class and

    43	// preserved byte-for-byte at both call sites (drawer header + status
    44	// `<dd>`) so the DOM shape stays `<span class="badge {variant}">
    45	// {label}</span>` and matches `SessionsTable.tsx` exactly.
    46	//
    47	// CSS lives in the sibling `SessionDetail.css` (drawer-body chrome +

    51	// recipes are in `SessionsTable.css`.

    53	import { ApiError, streamSessionRaw } from "../../lib/api";

    56	  consumeRawPreview,

    62	
    63	export type SessionDetailProps = {
    64	  /** The merged row to render. */
    65	  row: SessionRow;
    66	  /** Pinned-`now` ISO string used for relative-time labelling. */
    67	  now: string;
    68	};
    69	
    70	const COPY_HINT_TIMEOUT_MS = 2000;
    71	
    72	export function SessionDetail({ row, now }: SessionDetailProps) {
    73	  const sourcePath = row.sourcePath;
    74	  const sourcePathRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement | null>(null);
    75	  const [copyHint, setCopyHint] = useState<
    76	    "idle" | "copied" | "fallback"
    77	  >("idle");
    78	
    79	  // Reset the "Copied" / "Select to copy" hint after a short delay so
    80	  // it does not stay forever.
    81	  useEffect(() => {
    82	    if (copyHint === "idle") return;
    83	    const id = setTimeout(() => setCopyHint("idle"), COPY_HINT_TIMEOUT_MS);
    84	    return () => clearTimeout(id);
    85	  }, [copyHint]);
    86	
    87	  // Reset the hint when the row changes (the user opened a different
    88	  // drawer entry).
    89	  useEffect(() => {
    90	    setCopyHint("idle");
    91	  }, [row.rowKey]);
    92	
    93	  const handleCopyPath = async () => {
    94	    // Try the modern Clipboard API first. Browsers without it (older
    95	    // Chromium variants, locked-down test runners, jsdom-style shims)
    96	    // throw on call or simply do not expose the API; fall through to
    97	    // the manual-select fallback.
    98	    const clipboard = (
    99	      globalThis as unknown as {
   100	        navigator?: { clipboard?: { writeText?: (s: string) => Promise<void> } };
   101	      }
   102	    ).navigator?.clipboard;
   103	    if (clipboard?.writeText) {
   104	      try {
   105	        await clipboard.writeText(sourcePath);
   106	        setCopyHint("copied");
   107	        return;
   108	      } catch {
   109	        // Fall through to the manual-select fallback.
   110	      }
   111	    }
   112	    // Fallback: programmatically select the path text so the user can
   113	    // press Ctrl/Cmd + C themselves. We never throw — clipboard
   114	    // failure should not break the drawer.
   115	    const span = sourcePathRef.current;
   116	    if (span !== null) {
   117	      const range = document.createRange();
   118	      range.selectNodeContents(span);
   119	      const selection = (
   120	        globalThis as unknown as { getSelection?: () => Selection | null }
   121	      ).getSelection?.();
   122	      if (selection) {
   123	        selection.removeAllRanges();
   124	        selection.addRange(range);
   125	      }
   126	    }
   127	    setCopyHint("fallback");
   128	  };
   129	
   130	  const sourcePathLabel = row.sourcePathIsStale
   131	    ? "Last seen source path:"
   132	    : "Source path:";
   133	
   134	  // Only render the "View raw" anchor and the raw preview placeholder
   135	  // when the session is actually stored. A `source_only` row has no
   136	  // backend resource to view.
   137	  const rawHref =
   138	    row.storedSessionUid !== null
   139	      ? `/api/v1/sessions/${row.storedSessionUid}/raw`
   140	      : null;
   141	
   142	  return (
   143	    <div className="drawer-body">
   144	      <header className="drawer-header">
   145	        <h2 className="drawer-title">{row.title ?? "(untitled)"}</h2>
   146	        <span className="badge mono drawer-tool-badge">{row.tool}</span>
   147	        <span className={`badge ${row.status.replace(/_/g, "-")}`}>
   148	          {row.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   149	        </span>
   150	        {row.statusConflict ? (
   151	          <span
   152	            className="badge drawer-conflict-badge"
   153	            title="Source and stored status disagreed during load — refresh to re-fetch."
   154	          >
   155	            Conflict
   156	          </span>
   157	        ) : null}
   158	      </header>
   159	
   160	      <dl className="drawer-meta">
   161	        <dt>session_key</dt>
   162	        <dd className="mono">{row.sourceSessionKey ?? "—"}</dd>
   163	
   164	        <dt>session_uid</dt>
   165	        <dd className="mono">{row.storedSessionUid ?? "—"}</dd>
   166	
   167	        <dt>row_key</dt>
   168	        <dd className="mono">{row.rowKey}</dd>
   169	
   170	        <dt>tool</dt>
   171	        <dd className="mono">{row.tool}</dd>
   172	
   173	        <dt>source_session_id</dt>
   174	        <dd className="mono">{row.sourceSessionId}</dd>
   175	
   176	        <dt>presence</dt>
   177	        <dd className="mono">{row.presence}</dd>
   178	
   179	        <dt>status</dt>
   180	        <dd>
   181	          <span className={`badge ${row.status.replace(/_/g, "-")}`}>
   182	            {row.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   183	          </span>
   184	          {row.statusConflict ? (
   185	            <>
   186	              {" "}
   187	              <span className="muted">(disagreed during load)</span>
   188	            </>
   189	          ) : null}
   190	        </dd>
   191	
   192	        <dt>status_conflict</dt>
   193	        <dd className="mono">{row.statusConflict ? "true" : "false"}</dd>

     1	// Collapsible callout for persisted scan errors.
     2	//
     3	// Per `working/phase-4.md` §Phase 4 Goals → "6. Collapse what is
     4	// rarely non-empty", when the errors list is empty (the common case),
     5	// the component renders `null` — no header, no border, no whitespace.
     6	// This trades a small loss of "the panel is here, just empty" affordance
     7	// for a much cleaner page when nothing is wrong.
     8	//
     9	// When non-empty, the component renders:
    10	//   - a one-line summary explaining how many errors are recorded
    11	//   - a `<table>` with the same column vocabulary the previous
    12	//     always-rendered scan-errors panel used (Tool, Path, Message,
    13	//     Last Seen) so the existing test selectors and the visual cues
    14	//     users are used to stay valid
    15	//
    16	// Reuses the structural CSS selectors set by M1 (`.panel`, `.muted`,
    17	// `.mono`, `.table-wrap`). No feature-local CSS.
    18	import type { PersistedScanError } from "../lib/contracts";
    19	
    20	export type ScanErrorsCalloutProps = { errors: PersistedScanError[] };
    21	
    22	export function ScanErrorsCallout({ errors }: ScanErrorsCalloutProps) {
    23	  if (errors.length === 0) {
    24	    // M2 deliverable: collapse to nothing visible when the rare-non-empty
    25	    // list is empty. Returning `null` (vs an empty fragment) so React
    26	    // does not emit any DOM node at all.
    27	    return null;
    28	  }
    29	  const summary =
    30	    errors.length === 1
    31	      ? "1 scan error observed since the last rescan."
    32	      : `${errors.length} scan errors observed since the last rescan.`;
    33	  return (
    34	    <section className="panel" aria-label="Scan errors">
    35	      <h2>Scan Errors</h2>
    36	      <p className="muted">{summary}</p>
    37	      <div className="table-wrap">
    38	        <table>
    39	          <thead>
    40	            <tr>
    41	              <th>Tool</th>
    42	              <th>Path</th>
    43	              <th>Message</th>
    44	              <th>Last Seen</th>
    45	            </tr>
    46	          </thead>
    47	          <tbody>
    48	            {errors.map((error) => (
    49	              <tr key={error.error_id}>
    50	                <td className="mono">{error.tool}</td>
    51	                <td className="mono">{error.source_path}</td>
    52	                <td>{error.message}</td>
    53	                <td className="mono">{error.last_seen_at}</td>
    54	              </tr>
    55	            ))}
    56	          </tbody>
    57	        </table>
    58	      </div>
    59	    </section>
    60	  );
    61	}

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba apps/frontend/src/features/sessions/filterSessions.ts | sed -n '1,260p'" in /home/huwei/ai_codings/distill-portal
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Pure filter + sort helpers for the unified inspection list.
     2	//
     3	// `applyFilters` and `applySort` consume the merged `SessionRow[]` and
     4	// the persisted `SessionFiltersState`, then return a new array. Both
     5	// functions are pure (no input mutation, no React imports, no DOM
     6	// access) so they unit-test cheaply and can be memoized at the
     7	// `App.tsx` boundary.
     8	//
     9	// Per `working/phase-4.md` §Sort semantics:
    10	//   - Null handling: ascending sorts put nulls BEFORE non-null
    11	//     ("before non-null in ascending sorts"); descending sorts put
    12	//     nulls AFTER non-null ("after non-null values in descending
    13	//     sorts"). This matches the spec's exact wording.
    14	//   - Tiebreaker chain when the chosen sort field ties or both rows
    15	//     are null on the chosen field: apply the remaining timestamp
    16	//     fields in the FIXED order `sourceUpdatedAt → ingestedAt →
    17	//     createdAt`, skipping whichever one was the chosen field; then
    18	//     `title` (case-insensitive ASCII); then `rowKey` (always
    19	//     present, always unique). The chain is deterministic across
    20	//     renders.
    21	//   - Source clocks (`createdAt`, `sourceUpdatedAt`) come from the
    22	//     source machine and may be skewed. The drawer (M4) annotates
    23	//     these as "source clock"; M3's table is unannotated.
    24	//
    25	// Per spec §Filter, Sort, Search:
    26	//   - `tool: "all"` -> no tool filter; otherwise exact match.
    27	//   - `storage: "all"` -> no storage filter; "stored" -> rows with a
    28	//     stored copy; "not_stored" -> rows without one.
    29	//   - `status: []` AND `importableOnly === false` -> no status filter.
    30	//     `importableOnly === true` -> EFFECTIVE status = `["not_stored",
    31	//     "outdated"]` (overrides any leftover persisted array; the hook
    32	//     clears `status` when toggling importableOnly true, but the
    33	//     filter is also defensive against decode-path corruption).
    34	//   - `project: null` -> no filter; otherwise exact-match. The
    35	//     SessionFilters UI populates a `<datalist>` with distinct
    36	//     project paths from the current row set; the user picks one
    37	//     by autocomplete.
    38	//   - `search: ""` -> no filter; otherwise case-insensitive substring
    39	//     match against `title`, `sourceSessionId`, `sourcePath`,
    40	//     `projectPath`.
    41	import type { SessionSyncStatus } from "../../lib/contracts";
    42	import type { SessionRow } from "./types";
    43	import type { SessionFiltersState, SortField } from "./useSessionFilters";
    44	
    45	const IMPORTABLE_STATUSES: ReadonlyArray<SessionSyncStatus> = [
    46	  "not_stored",
    47	  "outdated",
    48	];
    49	
    50	/**
    51	 * Apply the filter axes to a merged row set. Returns a new array;
    52	 * input is never mutated.
    53	 *
    54	 * Multi-axis combinations are ANDed (a row must pass every active
    55	 * filter to survive). Inactive filters are skipped early so a default
    56	 * filter set is a fast-path no-op pass.
    57	 */
    58	export function applyFilters(
    59	  rows: SessionRow[],
    60	  filters: SessionFiltersState,
    61	): SessionRow[] {
    62	  // Effective status array. The hook clears `status` when
    63	  // `importableOnly` flips on, but recomputing here defends against
    64	  // a decode-path corruption that could leave both fields populated.
    65	  const effectiveStatus: SessionSyncStatus[] | null = filters.importableOnly
    66	    ? [...IMPORTABLE_STATUSES]
    67	    : filters.status.length > 0
    68	      ? [...filters.status]
    69	      : null;
    70	
    71	  const search = filters.search.trim();
    72	  const searchLower = search.length > 0 ? search.toLowerCase() : null;
    73	
    74	  return rows.filter((row) => {
    75	    if (filters.tool !== "all" && row.tool !== filters.tool) return false;
    76	
    77	    if (effectiveStatus !== null && !effectiveStatus.includes(row.status))
    78	      return false;
    79	
    80	    if (filters.storage === "stored" && row.storedSessionUid === null)
    81	      return false;
    82	    if (filters.storage === "not_stored" && row.storedSessionUid !== null)
    83	      return false;
    84	
    85	    if (filters.project !== null && row.projectPath !== filters.project)
    86	      return false;
    87	
    88	    if (searchLower !== null) {
    89	      const haystack = [
    90	        row.title ?? "",
    91	        row.sourceSessionId,
    92	        row.sourcePath,
    93	        row.projectPath ?? "",
    94	      ]
    95	        .join(" ")
    96	        .toLowerCase();
    97	      if (!haystack.includes(searchLower)) return false;
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    return true;
   101	  });
   102	}
   103	
   104	/**
   105	 * Sort a merged row set by the chosen field + direction with null
   106	 * handling and a deterministic tiebreaker chain.
   107	 *
   108	 * Returns a new array (`Array.prototype.slice().sort()`); input is
   109	 * never mutated.
   110	 */
   111	export function applySort(
   112	  rows: SessionRow[],
   113	  sort: SessionFiltersState["sort"],
   114	): SessionRow[] {
   115	  const out = rows.slice();
   116	  const directionMul = sort.direction === "asc" ? 1 : -1;
   117	
   118	  out.sort((a, b) => {
   119	    const primary = compareField(a, b, sort.field, sort.direction);
   120	    if (primary !== 0) return primary;
   121	    // Tiebreak: apply remaining timestamp fields in fixed order, then
   122	    // title (case-insensitive), then rowKey. The tiebreaker direction
   123	    // matches the user's chosen direction so a deterministic order
   124	    // emerges; the rowKey final tiebreaker is always ascending so two
   125	    // rows with identical timestamps and titles still settle the same
   126	    // way regardless of input ordering.
   127	    for (const tField of TIEBREAK_TIMESTAMPS) {
   128	      if (tField === sort.field) continue;
   129	      const t = compareField(a, b, tField, sort.direction);
   130	      if (t !== 0) return t;
   131	    }
   132	    if (sort.field !== "title") {
   133	      const ta = (a.title ?? "").toLowerCase();
   134	      const tb = (b.title ?? "").toLowerCase();
   135	      if (ta < tb) return -1 * directionMul;
   136	      if (ta > tb) return 1 * directionMul;
   137	    }
   138	    if (a.rowKey < b.rowKey) return -1;
   139	    if (a.rowKey > b.rowKey) return 1;
   140	    return 0;
   141	  });
   142	
   143	  return out;
   144	}
   145	
   146	const TIEBREAK_TIMESTAMPS: ReadonlyArray<SortField> = [
   147	  "source_updated_at",
   148	  "ingested_at",
   149	  "created_at",
   150	];
   151	
   152	/**
   153	 * Compare two rows on a single field, honoring the spec's null rule:
   154	 *   - Ascending: nulls BEFORE non-null (smaller).
   155	 *   - Descending: nulls AFTER non-null (larger).
   156	 *
   157	 * Returns the standard <0/0/>0 contract for the user's chosen
   158	 * direction, with the null rule applied symmetrically — the
   159	 * caller's `Array.sort` does not need to know about nulls.
   160	 */
   161	function compareField(
   162	  a: SessionRow,
   163	  b: SessionRow,
   164	  field: SortField,
   165	  direction: "asc" | "desc",
   166	): number {
   167	  const va = readField(a, field);
   168	  const vb = readField(b, field);
   169	  // Null handling: ascending puts nulls first; descending puts them last.
   170	  if (va === null && vb === null) return 0;
   171	  if (va === null) return direction === "asc" ? -1 : 1;
   172	  if (vb === null) return direction === "asc" ? 1 : -1;
   173	  // Both non-null: lexicographic compare (works for ISO 8601
   174	  // RFC 3339 UTC timestamps AND for plain strings — both monotonic
   175	  // under string compare). Direction multiplier gives the user's
   176	  // chosen orientation.
   177	  const directionMul = direction === "asc" ? 1 : -1;
   178	  if (field === "title") {
   179	    const la = (va as string).toLowerCase();
   180	    const lb = (vb as string).toLowerCase();
   181	    if (la < lb) return -1 * directionMul;
   182	    if (la > lb) return 1 * directionMul;
   183	    return 0;
   184	  }
   185	  if (va < vb) return -1 * directionMul;
   186	  if (va > vb) return 1 * directionMul;
   187	  return 0;
   188	}
   189	
   190	function readField(row: SessionRow, field: SortField): string | null {
   191	  switch (field) {
   192	    case "source_updated_at":
   193	      return row.sourceUpdatedAt;
   194	    case "created_at":
   195	      return row.createdAt;
   196	    case "ingested_at":
   197	      return row.ingestedAt;
   198	    case "title":
   199	      return row.title;
   200	    case "project_path":
   201	      return row.projectPath;
   202	  }
   203	}
   204	
   205	/**
   206	 * Distinct, non-null project paths across the row set. The
   207	 * SessionFilters UI feeds this into a `<datalist>` so the user can
   208	 * pick a project via autocomplete. Sorted ASCII-ascending for stable
   209	 * dropdown ordering.
   210	 */
   211	export function distinctProjectPaths(rows: SessionRow[]): string[] {
   212	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   213	  for (const row of rows) {
   214	    if (row.projectPath !== null) seen.add(row.projectPath);
   215	  }
   216	  return Array.from(seen).sort();
   217	}

     5	//
     6	//   (1)  Round-trip: persist a custom shape, decode it back, every
     7	//        field matches.
     8	//   (2)  Malformed JSON in the persisted blob -> defaults.
     9	//   (3)  null top-level -> defaults.
    10	//   (4)  Array top-level -> defaults.
    11	//   (5)  String top-level -> defaults.
    12	//   (6)  Missing top-level keys -> per-field defaults; valid keys

    14	//   (7)  Unknown `tool` enum value -> default.

    16	//   (9)  Non-array `status` -> default `[]`.

   169	// (7b) Unknown `storage` enum value -> default. Mirrors (7) for the
   170	//      sibling enum; covers the second `VALID_STORAGES` membership
   171	//      check in the decoder.
   172	test("decodeFilters: unknown storage enum falls back to default", () => {
   173	  const out = decodeFilters(JSON.stringify({ storage: "vim" }));
   174	  expect(out.storage).toBe(DEFAULT_FILTERS.storage);
   175	});
   176

   178	test("decodeFilters: unknown sort.field falls back to default sort", () => {
   179	  const out = decodeFilters(
   180	    JSON.stringify({ sort: { field: "nope", direction: "asc" } }),
   181	  );
   182	  expect(out.sort.field).toBe(DEFAULT_FILTERS.sort.field);
   183	  // `direction` was valid; spec robustness rule applies per-key, but
   184	  // the implementation always returns a coherent sort object — when
   185	  // field is invalid the whole field defaults; direction is taken
   186	  // from the persisted value if valid. Either is acceptable; assert
   187	  // both for explicit coverage.
   188	  expect(out.sort.direction).toBe("asc");
   189	});

   195	  const out = decodeFilters(

     1	// Tests for `useSelectedSession` — the `?session=<rowKey>` URL hook.
     2	//
     3	// Coverage (per chunk dispatch brief):
     4	//   - initial mount reads URLSearchParams.get("session") and pre-
     5	//     selects
     6	//   - initial mount with no `?session=` param: selectedRowKey === null
     7	//   - selectRow(key) updates state AND calls replaceState with
     8	//     `?session=key`
     9	//   - selectRow(null) removes the session param while preserving
    10	//     other query params (e.g. `?foo=bar`)
    11	//   - popstate event re-reads URL and syncs selectedRowKey without
    12	//     calling replaceState
    13	//   - buildUrl preserves all other query params verbatim
    14	//   - popstate listener cleaned up on unmount
    15	//
    16	// Uses happy-dom (preloaded by `bunfig.toml`) for window.location +
    17	// window.history + URLSearchParams. We mutate location.search via
    18	// `history.replaceState` (the browser-correct way; happy-dom
    19	// supports it) and dispatch synthetic `PopStateEvent`s on `window`.
    20	import { afterEach, beforeEach, expect, test } from "bun:test";
    21	import { act, cleanup, renderHook } from "@testing-library/react";
    22	import { useSelectedSession, buildUrl } from "./useSelectedSession";
    23	
    24	afterEach(() => {
    25	  cleanup();
    26	  // Reset URL between tests so leftover state doesn't bleed.
    27	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/");
    28	});
    29	
    30	beforeEach(() => {
    31	  // Same — reset before each test for symmetry.
    32	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/");
    33	});
    34	
    35	test("useSelectedSession: initial mount with no ?session= → selectedRowKey is null", () => {
    36	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/");
    37	  const { result } = renderHook(() => useSelectedSession());
    38	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBeNull();
    39	});
    40	
    41	test("useSelectedSession: initial mount reads URLSearchParams.get('session') and pre-selects", () => {
    42	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?session=foo-key");
    43	  const { result } = renderHook(() => useSelectedSession());
    44	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBe("foo-key");
    45	});
    46	
    47	test("useSelectedSession: selectRow(key) updates state AND calls replaceState with ?session=key", () => {
    48	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/");
    49	  const { result } = renderHook(() => useSelectedSession());
    50	  act(() => {
    51	    result.current.selectRow("alpha");
    52	  });
    53	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBe("alpha");
    54	  expect(window.location.search).toBe("?session=alpha");
    55	});
    56	
    57	test("useSelectedSession: selectRow(null) removes the session param while preserving other query params", () => {
    58	  // Pre-seed the URL with both `session` and an unrelated `foo` key.
    59	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?foo=bar&session=alpha");
    60	  const { result } = renderHook(() => useSelectedSession());
    61	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBe("alpha");
    62	  act(() => {
    63	    result.current.selectRow(null);
    64	  });
    65	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBeNull();
    66	  // `?foo=bar` is preserved; `session` is gone.
    67	  const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    68	  expect(params.get("foo")).toBe("bar");
    69	  expect(params.has("session")).toBe(false);
    70	});
    71	
    72	test("useSelectedSession: popstate event re-reads URL and syncs selectedRowKey", () => {
    73	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?session=initial");
    74	  const { result } = renderHook(() => useSelectedSession());
    75	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBe("initial");
    76	
    77	  // Simulate a Back/Forward navigation: change the URL THEN dispatch
    78	  // popstate. Order matters — popstate's handler re-reads
    79	  // window.location.search.
    80	  act(() => {
    81	    window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?session=after-back");
    82	    const PopStateEventCtor =
    83	      (globalThis as unknown as { window: { PopStateEvent: typeof PopStateEvent } })
    84	        .window.PopStateEvent;
    85	    window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEventCtor("popstate"));
    86	  });
    87	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBe("after-back");
    88	});
    89	
    90	test("useSelectedSession: popstate handler does NOT call replaceState (no feedback loop)", () => {
    91	  // Spy on history.replaceState. The popstate handler should ONLY
    92	  // call setState — calling replaceState from the popstate path
    93	  // would feedback-loop with the browser's own URL push.
    94	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?session=initial");
    95	  const { result } = renderHook(() => useSelectedSession());
    96	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBe("initial");
    97	
    98	  const original = window.history.replaceState.bind(window.history);
    99	  let calls = 0;
   100	  window.history.replaceState = ((
   101	    state: unknown,
   102	    title: string,
   103	    url?: string | null,
   104	  ) => {
   105	    calls += 1;
   106	    return original(state, title, url ?? null);
   107	  }) as typeof window.history.replaceState;
   108	
   109	  try {
   110	    act(() => {
   111	      // Update the URL directly (simulating the browser's own update
   112	      // for Back/Forward) and then dispatch popstate.
   113	      original(null, "", "/?session=via-popstate");
   114	      const PopStateEventCtor =
   115	        (globalThis as unknown as { window: { PopStateEvent: typeof PopStateEvent } })
   116	          .window.PopStateEvent;
   117	      window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEventCtor("popstate"));
   118	    });
   119	    // The popstate handler should not have called the spy at all.
   120	    expect(calls).toBe(0);
   121	    expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBe("via-popstate");
   122	  } finally {
   123	    window.history.replaceState = original;
   124	  }
   125	});
   126	
   127	test("buildUrl preserves all other query params verbatim", () => {
   128	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?foo=bar&baz=qux");
   129	  // Setting session="x" should keep foo + baz.
   130	  const url1 = buildUrl("x");
   131	  const params1 = new URLSearchParams(url1.split("?")[1] ?? "");
   132	  expect(params1.get("foo")).toBe("bar");
   133	  expect(params1.get("baz")).toBe("qux");
   134	  expect(params1.get("session")).toBe("x");
   135	
   136	  // Removing session entirely must leave the other keys intact.
   137	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?foo=bar&baz=qux&session=x");
   138	  const url2 = buildUrl(null);
   139	  const params2 = new URLSearchParams(url2.split("?")[1] ?? "");
   140	  expect(params2.get("foo")).toBe("bar");
   141	  expect(params2.get("baz")).toBe("qux");
   142	  expect(params2.has("session")).toBe(false);
   143	});
   144	
   145	test("useSelectedSession: popstate listener cleaned up on unmount", () => {
   146	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", "/?session=initial");
   147	  const { result, unmount } = renderHook(() => useSelectedSession());
   148	  expect(result.current.selectedRowKey).toBe("initial");
   149	
   150	  // Unmount; subsequent popstate events must NOT mutate state of an
   151	  // unmounted component. We verify this via the "no warnings about
   152	  // setting state on unmounted component" approach — attempt the
   153	  // dispatch and make sure no error throws (happy-dom does not warn,
   154	  // but the listener's removeEventListener must have been called).
   155	  // The cleanest assertion is to spy on removeEventListener.
   156	  const removeEventListenerSpy = (() => {
   157	    let called: { type: string; fn: EventListener } | null = null;
   158	    const original = window.removeEventListener.bind(window);
   159	    window.removeEventListener = ((
   160	      type: string,
   161	      fn: EventListenerOrEventListenerObject,
   162	      options?: boolean | EventListenerOptions,
   163	    ) => {
   164	      if (type === "popstate") {
   165	        called = { type, fn: fn as EventListener };
   166	      }
   167	      return original(
   168	        type,
   169	        fn,
   170	        options as boolean | EventListenerOptions | undefined,
   171	      );
   172	    }) as typeof window.removeEventListener;
   173	    return {
   174	      called: () => called,
   175	      restore: () => {
   176	        window.removeEventListener = original;
   177	      },
   178	    };
   179	  })();
   180	
   181	  try {
   182	    unmount();
   183	    expect(removeEventListenerSpy.called()).not.toBeNull();
   184	  } finally {
   185	    removeEventListenerSpy.restore();
   186	  }
